nūṟ kuṟippu

		kaḷappirar kālat tamiḻaka varalāṟu

mayili cīṉi vēṅkaṭacāmi,
mu. aruṇācalam, naṭaṉa. kācinātaṉ

mutaṟpatippu	: 	2008

“periyār kuṭil”
pi-11, kulmōkar kuṭiyiruppu, 
15, teṟku pōkku cāli, tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉi - 600 017

kaḷappirar kālat tamiḻaka varalāṟu 
(ki.pi.300-600)
nam nāṭṭu varalāṟṟilum cari, piṟanāṭṭu varalāṟu
kaḷilum cari, cila nilappakutikaḷiṉ cila kāla varalāṟṟu nikaḻccikaḷ kuṟittu ōraḷavukkākavatu teḷivāka virivākak kūṟavataṟkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ kūṭak kiṭippatilli. akkālaṅkaḷic cilar iruṇṭa kālaṅkaḷ eṉpar. cila eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ varumāṟu:
i. ki.mu.1700 kkup piṉ cintuveḷit tol tamiḻa
nākarikam ēṉ muṟṟilum aḻintatu? eppaṭi?
ii. cīṉāvil cu parampariyiṉar āṇṭa ki.mu. 900-300 kālattil ciṟṟaracar mikappalar āṇṭaṉar. āṉāl vivaraṅkaḷ teriyavilli.
iii. vaṭaamerikkāvil mekcikōvil ki.pi. 3-9 nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil ciṟantu viḷaṅkiya mayā nākarikam ataṉpiṉ aṭiyōṭu aḻivuṟṟami 
tamiḻakavaralāṟṟil ki.pi. 300- 600 kālattiyum attakiya iruṇṭa kālamākap palarum karutukiṉṟaṉar.
2. kaḷappirar kālat tamiḻaka varalāṟṟip paṟṟi virivāṉa nūleḻutiyuḷḷavarkaḷ irā.paṉṉīr celvam (1973); mayili cīṉi vēṅkaṭacāmi (1975); mu.aruṇācalam (1979); naṭaṉakācinātaṉ (1981) ākiyōr āvar. piṟar eḻutiyuḷḷa ciṟiya kaṭṭurikaḷ pala; vēṟu poruḷ nutaliya pala varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷilum kaḷappirar kāla nikaḻccikaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; avaṟṟiṉ vivaraṅkaḷiyellām in nāṉku nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇalām. mūṉṟu nūlkaḷ appaṭiyē acciṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aruṇācalattiṉ āṅkila nūlukku tamiḻc curukkam tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
3. caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kuṟittuḷḷa pāṇṭiya cōḻa cēra maṉṉaruḷ mikap piṟpaṭṭavarkaḷ kālam ki.pi. 250 - 300 aḷavil muṭivuṟṟatu. piṟkālap pāṇṭiyar āṭci ki.pi.560- 590 mutal āṇṭa kaṭuṅkōṉ kālam mutal toṭaṅkiyatu. piṟkālac cōḻar āṭci vicayālayaṉ (ki.pi.848 - 881) mutal toṭaṅkiyatu. pallava, pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ āḷukiyil cōḻanāṭuki.pi. 848varit toṭarntatāl tāṉ cōḻa parampari ki.pi. 600 liruntē pāṇṭiyarip pōla mīṇṭum uyir peṟa iyalavilli.
4. kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭavarākiya ciravaṇapukoḷa vaṭṭārattu kaḷippu nāṭṭuk kaḷappirar (=kaḷappāḷar) ēṟattāḻa ki.pi. 250l allatu ataṟkuc caṟṟup piṉṉar tamiḻakattik kippaṟṟi pāṇṭiya cōḻa cēra nāṭukaḷi ki.pi. 575 vari āṇṭaṉar eṉpar ma.cī. veṅkaṭacāmi. ka.a. nīlakaṇṭa cāttiri, na. cuppiramaṇiyaṉ ākiyōrum ikkaruttiṉarē. avarkaḷ kaṉṉaṭar eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ varumāṟu.
‘kāṉak kaṭicūḻvaṭukak karunāṭar maṉṉaṉ’
- tiruttoṇṭar purāṇam - mūrtti nāyaṉār purāṇam.
“maturi vavviya karunaṭar vēntaṉ” -kallāṭam
5. ki.pi. 574 ai oṭṭi kaḷappirarimiṭaruntu pāṇṭiya nāṭṭip pāṇṭiyaṉ kaṭuṅkōṉ mīṇṭum kippaṟṟiṉāṉ.
“ aḷavariya ātirājari akalanīkki akaliṭattik
kaḷapraṉeṉṉuṅ kali aricaṉ kikkoṇṭataṉi iṟakkiyapiṉ
................. kaṭuṅkōṉeṉṉuṅ katirvēṟṟeṉṉaṉ”
- parāntaka neṭuñcaṭiyaṉ (ki.pi 756-815)
uṭiya vēḷvikkuṭi ceppēṭu.
“kaḷappāḻarik kaḷikaṭṭa maṟṟiraṇṭōḷ
mākkaṭuṅ kōṉ”
- parāntaka vīranārāyaṇaṉ (860-905) taḷavāypuram ceppēṭu.
taṅkaḷik kaḷappirar tōṟkaṭitta veṟuppil pāṇṭiyar aṉṉārik kaliyaricar (tīya aracar) eṉa ikkalveṭṭil paḻittaṉar pōlum; mēlum kaḷappirarukku camaṇa - puttarkaḷōṭu irunta toṭarpukaḷit tamiḻaka varalāṟṟāciriyar mikippaṭuttikkūṟu kiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē tāṉ kaḷappirar “tīya aracar” ākkappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉar eṉpār parṭaṉ sṭeyṉ.camaṇamata untutalāl tāṉ paṇṭit tamiḻ. vēntarik kaḷappirar veṉṟaṉar; pirāmaṇiya niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷukku ikkaṭṭu ēṟpaṭṭatu eṉṟu nīlakaṇṭa cāttiri kūṟuvataṟkum ātāram illi eṉpar sṭeyṉ. ‘All the King’s Mana: perspectives on medieval South India’ July 1974 Seminar paper published in J.F. Richard ( King Ship and authority in South Asia University of Wisconsin; Madison 1978/81).
ki.pi. 575 ai oṭṭi kaḷappirariṭamiruntu cōḻa
nāṭṭi pallava cimmaviṣṇu kippaṟṟik koṇṭāṉ.
6. piṟkālattil muttariyar eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiya kuṟunilamaṉṉarum kaḷappirarum potuvāṉa oru kuṭiyiliruntu pirintu vaḷarntavar eṉpār naṭaṉa. kācinātaṉ (1976 “muttariyar”)
7. mēlē 4 ām pattiyiṟ kaṇṭataṟku māṟāka, kaḷappirar tamiḻarē eṉṟu karutiyavar es. kiruṣṇacāmi aiyaṅkār, 
mu. irākaviyaṅkār, pi.ṭi. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār, ka.pa. aṟavāṇaṉ mutaliyōr āvar. karunāṭakattil ciravaṇa pukuḷa pakutiyiliruntu tamiḻakam pukunta vantēṟikaḷē kaḷappirar eṉpatu kē.ār. veṅkaṭarāmaṉ (1956-57 Journal of Indian History XXXIV Part II paṉṉīr celvam, vēṅkaṭacāmi, naṭaṉa kācinātaṉ, aruṇācalam pōṉṟōr karuttākum. piṉṉavarkaḷ karuttē ituvarik kiṭittuḷḷa ātāraṅkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil amintatākat tōṉṟukiṟatu eṉpati innūṟṟokuppip paṭippavarkaḷ uṇarvar.
8. pi.ji.el. cuvāmi 1976 ‘Kalabhra Interregnum’ a retropect and prospect Bulletin of the Institute of Traditional cultures; Madras (pages 81 - 148) karuttu “kaḷappirar paṭiyeṭuppu” eṉpatu ki.pi. 8 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil (kaṉṉaṭiya) kaṅka aracarkaḷuṭiya orupaṭi tiṇṭukkallukkum maturikkum iṭippaṭṭatāṉa pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ oru ciṟu nilappakutiyik kippaṟṟi oru cila āṇṭukaḷ āṇṭatik kuṟippatutāṉ eṉpa tākum!
kaḷappirar camayam
9. kaḷappirar camayam perumpālum camaṇam; cōḻa nāṭṭik kippaṟṟiyavarkaḷ maṭṭum puttam eṉa vēṅkaṭacāmiyum aruṇācalamum kāṭṭum ātāraṅkaḷ maṟukkak kūṭiyaviyākat tōṉṟavilli. tiruñāṉacampantarum tirunāvukkaracarum - kuṟippāka muṉṉavar - camaṇa putta camayaṅkaḷit tam tēvārap patikaṅkaḷil kaṭumiyāṉa vacavu moḻikaḷāl tākkuvatum, camaṇarik ki.pi. 6 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil kaḻuvēṟṟiya ceytikaḷi cēkkiḻārum piṟarum cātaṉiyāka makiḻcciyuṭaṉ terivippatum mukkiyamāṉa ātāramākum.
kaḷappirar kālattil tamiḻ ilakkiyam, kalikaḷ
10. kaḷappirar tāymoḻi kaṉṉaṭam. (avarkaḷ kālattil camaṇamum puttamum araciṉ ātaravuṭaṉ vaḷarntirukka vēṇṭum.) eṉavē ki.pi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil ivviru camayaṅkaḷi oḻikka ñāṉa campantar, nāvukkaracar pōṉṟōr pala koṭumikaḷic ceyya vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. eṉiṉum tamiḻmoḻi ilakkiyat tuṟikaḷil kaḷappirarkaḷāl perumaḷavukku pātippu iruntirukkātu eṉpar vēṅkaṭacāmi patiṉeṇ kīḻk kaṇakku nūlkaḷukku avar tarum kālavariyaṟi varumāṟu :
tirukkuṟaḷ, kaḷavaḻi nāṟpatu, ki.pi.250kku muṟpaṭṭa
mutumoḻik kāñci caṅkakāla nūlkaḷ
nālaṭiyār - ki.pi. 7 m nūṟṟāṇṭu
ēṉiya 14 nūlkaḷum - ki.pi. 3- 6 nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ
kaḷappirar āṭciyil camaṇa, putta camayaṅkaḷ ciṟap
paṭintiruntaṉa. eṉavē ki.pi. 600kkup piṉṉar civa viṇava camayaṅkaḷ taḻitta poḻutu kaḷappirar kālattiya camaṇa, putta camayam cārnta tamiḻkaḷ pala aḻikkappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉpar vēṅkaṭacāmi. tiruñāṉa campantar camaṇaruṭaṉ nikaḻttiya aṉalvāta, puṉalvātac ceytikaḷ itik kuṟippaṉavēyeṉka.
11. māṟākak kaḷappirar kālattil tamiḻ moḻiyum ilakkiyamum tākkuṇṭaṉa; piṉṉar ki.pi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil civa camayattiṉ ki ōṅkiya piṉṉarē tamiḻ paḻiya niliyip peṟṟatu eṉpatu aruṇācalattiṉ nili ākum. avarukku muṉṉar itu paṟṟiya eḻutiya catāciva paṇṭārattār, oḷavi turicāmippiḷḷi pōṉṟavarkaḷ karuttum ituvē. potuvāka varalāṟu paṟṟiya aṟiñarkaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷi -kīḻē 14 - 15 pattikaḷ kāṇpavarkaḷukku ivviru vakiyiṉariṉ muraṇpaṭṭa nili viyappit tarātu. ici, nāṭakam mutaliyaṉa vāḻntaṉa vaḷarntaṉa eṉpār vēṅkaṭacāmi; vīḻntaṉa eṉpār aruṇācalam!
12. ivviṭattil piṉvarum karuttukaḷi niṉivu kūṟalām cārlas ī kōvar : teṉṉintiya nāṭṭār pāṭalkaḷ (The Folk songs of Southern India) 1871 : tiṭṭamiṭṭu citikkappaṭāta allatu māṟṟi yeḻutappaṭāta tolpaḻaṅkālat tamiḻ. nūl etiyum iṉṟu kāṇpatu aritu. tamiḻ makkaḷ - tirāviṭa (paḻantamiḻ) ilakkiyattik kiviṭṭu purāṇakkatikaḷi nampiṉāltāṉ taṅkaḷukkup piḻippu uṇṭu eṉṟu uṇarntavarkaḷē ivvāṟu paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyattic citittavarkaḷ āvar. (taṉit) tamiḻc ceyyuḷ nūlkaḷ pala matippiḻantu oḻiyumpaṭi avarkaḷ ceytaṉar. aṟavē oḻikka muṭiyāta nērvukaḷil annūlkaḷi ayōkkiyattaṉamāka citittu urumāṟṟi ulavaviṭṭu mūlanūliṉ karutti uṇara muṭiyātavāṟu ceytu viṭṭaṉar.
hayūnevil : ti tapropēṉiyaṉ ( The Taprobanian) cūṉ 1896 “maturit tamiḻc caṅkamum ataṉ nūlakamum aḻikkappaṭṭa poḻutu paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷ cakaṭṭumēṉikku aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. (avaṟṟuḷ camaṇak koḷki vāṭi irukkalām eṉṟa karuttil). īḻattil makāvamcam iruntatu pōl pāṇṭiyarkaḷ varalāṟṟik kūṟum oru nūl maturic caṅka nūlkaḷil oṉṟāka iruntu aḻikkap
paṭṭu ataṟkup patilāka tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇam uruvākkappaṭṭu irukka vēṇṭum. aḻikkappaṭṭa varalāṟṟu nūliṉ citivukaḷuṭaṉ, aiṅkamarkaḷ (pārppaṉiya civa matattavarkaḷ) eṇṇam, camayam ākiyavaṟṟukkup poruttamāṉa puṉikatikaḷuṭaṉ piṉṉip piṇintu appurāṇam uruvākkappaṭṭatu.” 
mu.rā. perumāḷ mutaliyār “ēṭṭaḷavil niṉṟu viṭṭa kuṟaḷ neṟi” kaṭṭuriyil (centamiḻc celvi 59:5 caṉavari 1985; pak 130-4) kūṟum karuttu poruḷ potintatākavē uḷḷatu.
“pārppaṉar tamatu mēmpāṭṭi iḻantaṉar eṉpatāl tāṉ akkālatti iruṇṭakālam eṉṟu colkiṉṟavar. tirukkuṟaḷum cilappatikāramum eḻutappaṭṭa kālam iruṇṭa kālamāy irukka muṭiyumā?”
13. vēṅkaṭacāmi nūliṉ iṇippu “i. iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷ - varalāṟṟu āyvu” eṉṉum pakutiyil kaṇṭa karuttu poruttamākavē tōṉṟukiṟatu.
14. parṭaṉ sṭeyṉ tamatu 1978 āyvukkaṭṭuriyil kūṟum maṟṟoru karuttum mukkiyamāṉatu:- kaḷappirar ātikkam muṭintavuṭaṉ tamiḻnāṭṭil camaṇamatattiṉ kati muṭintu viṭṭatu pōla tamiḻnāṭṭiṉarāṉa varalāṟṟāciriyar cuḷuvākak karutiviṭukiṉṟaṉar, makēntiravarmaṉ, pāṇṭiya vēntaṉ ākiya perumaṉṉar matamāka irunta ataṉi avvaḷavu cīkkiramāka oḻittuk kaṭṭiyirukka
muṭiyātu; aracavikaḷil celvākku pōyviṭṭālum vēṟu pala tuṟikaḷilum (paṇpāṭu ilakkiyam uṭpaṭa) tamiḻnāṭṭil camaṇac celvākku mēlum cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ valuvāka iruntirukkavēṇṭum. āntira, karunāṭakap pakutikaḷil accelvākku mēlum cilanūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ nīṭittatu; eṉpār avar. iṟutiyāka varalāṟu paṟṟiya aṟiñarulakattiṉ karutti innūliṟ paṭippōr pārvikkukkoṇṭu vara ivvāyppip payaṉpaṭuttik koḷkiṟōm.
varalāṟu cila potuk karuttukkaḷ:
15. iṟutiyāka, varalāṟu paṟṟiya aṟiñarulakattiṉ karutti innūlip paṭippōr pārvikkuk koṇṭu vara ivvāyppip payaṉpaṭuttik koḷkiṟōm.
varalāṟu eṉpatu eṉṉa? muṟkālattil eṉṉa naṭantirukkalām eṉpatip paṟṟi pintiya talimuṟiyic cārnta māntaṉ oruvaṉ taṉatu maṉattil uruvākkit koḷḷum eṇṇamē atu. eḻutupavaṉuṭiya aṟivunili. maṉanili, avaṉuṭiya camutāyap pārvi, avaṉukkut kiṭṭum ātāraṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟukkēṟpavē oru kālattip paṟṟi allatu oru poruḷippaṟṟi oruvaṉ varalāṟu eḻutukiṟāṉ. eṉavē ovvoru kālam, poruḷ paṟṟi palvēṟu varalāṟukaḷ irukkak kūṭiyaṉavē. 
varalāṟṟāciriyaṉ paṭṭaṟivu, paṟpala viṣayaṅkaḷip paṟṟiya avaṉuṭiya kaṇṇōṭṭam ākiyavaṟṟāl uruvāṉa avaṉuṭiya maṉam tāṉ avaṉ eppaṭi varalāṟṟi eḻutukiṟāṉ eṉpati nirṇayikkiṟatu. viruppu veṟuppaṟṟa varalāṟṟāciriyaṉ muyaṟkompu tāṉ. eṉavē enta varalāṟṟu nūlum muḻumiyāṉa appaṭṭamāṉa uṇmiyik kār uḷḷaḷavum kaṭal nīr uḷḷaḷavum nirṇayittu viṭṭatāka eṇṇiviṭak kūṭātu.
varalāṟṟāciriyaṉ allatu avaṉip payaṉpaṭuttupavarkaḷ uḷḷattil veḷippaṭiyākavō (allatu avarkaḷukkē teriyāmal āḻmaṉattilō) uḷḷa kuṟikkōḷkaḷuṭaṉtāṉ varalāṟu eḻutappaṭukiṟatu. akkuṟik kōḷkaḷ piṟa iṉaṅkaḷi, kuḻukkaḷik kaṭṭuppaṭuttutalum vacappaṭuttutalum; camutāyattiṟku ilakku
kaḷik kāṭṭi ūkkuvittal; kuḻukkaḷ, varkkaṅkaḷukku uṇarcci yūṭṭutal, atikāratti ēṟkeṉavē kiyiṟ koṇṭuḷḷavarkaḷukku valuvūṭṭutal; atikāramillātavarkaḷiṭiyē ‘ippoḻutuḷḷa nilimiyē cari, eṉṉum pontiki maṉaniliyi ēṟpaṭuttutal pōṉṟaṉavām. 
āḷupavarkaḷ, aracukaḷ cārpāka eḻutappaṭum varalāṟukaḷi nampuvatu kolikāraṉ colvati appaṭiyē nampuvatu pōla.
varalāṟṟi maṟappavarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ paṭṭa iṉṉal
kaḷi iṭiviṭātu mīṇṭum peṟuvar. 
ippattikkāṉa āṅkila mūlaṅkaḷ varumāṟu:
History is a reconstruction of elements of the past in the mind of a human being of a later generation..... In principle there will be multiple histories of any given period, each congruent to the mental world, social purposes, and sources avilable to the person who creates it. **
Since the recreation of the past takes place in the mind of the individual historian which has been shaped by his personal experience and world view the unbiased historian is an unattinable ideal. ***
By the very nature of the historical discourse there can be no final truth - Gary Bechman “Thd limits of credulity Journal of the American Oriental Society 125.3 (2005)”
(“History”) is always a created ideology with a purpose, designed to control individuals or motivate societies, or inspire classes... to strengthen the purpose of those who possessed power... and reconcile those who lacked it.
- J.H. Plumb (1969) The death of the past (quoted by Beckman)
official history is believing the murderers at their word 
- Simone Weil
Those who disregard the past are bound to repeat it - George Santayana
16, kaḷappirar āṭci nallatā, keṭṭatā eṉṟu veṭṭu oṉṟu tuṇṭu iraṇṭāka inta muṉṉuri kūṟavilliyē eṉak karutalām! ittakiya cikkalāṉa poruṇmikaḷil appaṭik kūṟa iyalātu, kūṟuvatum cariyalla eṉpatē ciṟanta varalāṟṟaṟiñar karuttu ākum. ilaṭcakkaṇakkāṉa āvaṇaṅkaḷum pala āyirak kaṇakkāṉa ātāra nūlkaḷum 1789 pireñcuppuraṭci paṟṟi irukkiṉṟaṉa. iruntum appuraṭciyiṉ tōṟṟam, viḷivukaḷip paṟṟi iṉṟiya niliyilum kūṭak kaṟārāṉa muṭivukaḷik kūṟa iyalātu eṉpar varalāṟṟiñar ji.em. ṭirevelyaṉ :-
“ittakiya mika mukkiyamāṉa kaṭuñcikkalāṉa viṣayaṅkaḷip paṟṟi “itu tāṉ varalāṟṟiṉ tīrppu” eṉṟu entak kālattilum kaṟārākak kūṟiviṭa iyalātu”
“ [ittakiya viṣayaṅkaḷil]” kiṭittuḷḷa varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟukaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil avaravar tamakkut tāmē cintittu ōraḷavukku oru muṭivukkum vara iyalum. appaṭic cintittu muṭivukku vara muyalvatu namatu cintaṉiyāṟṟalukkuc ciṟanta payiṟciyākum; nam aṉivarukkum iṉṟiyamiyāta payiṟciyum kūṭa.”
“varalāṟṟip payilvataṉ mūlam mānta iṉattiṉ aṉittup piracṉikaḷiyum akaṉṟa nuṇṇiya pārviyuṭaṉum amiti yākavum cintittu muṭivu ceyyum tiṟaṉum vaḻakkamum namakku varum. paṇṭiya varalāṟṟuc ceytikaḷi ellāk kōṇaṅkaḷi liruntum amitiyāka eṇṇip pārttu eṭi pōṭum poḻutu maṉam namatu aṉṟāṭap piracciṉikaḷik kūrnta nuṇṇaṟivuṭaṉ purintu koḷḷum tiṟaṉi eytum. mānta iṉattiṉ iṭiyāppac cikkalkaḷic ceytit tāḷ tirippaṭam [ippoḻutu, ivaṟṟiyum viṭa mika mōcamāṉa tollik kāṭciyiyum cērttuk koḷḷalām] ākiya pullaṟi vāṇmimikka ūṭakaṅkaḷ arivēkkāṭṭut taṉamāka (ēṉ 1/100 vēkkāṭṭut taṉamākak kūṭa) “eḷimippaṭutti” parappuri ceytu takiṭutattam paṇṇuvatiliruntu nammik kāttukkoḷḷa attiṟaṉ utavum.”
“On such great and complex issues there can never be a final verdict of history. But at least it is possible to have an opinion of some value. and the attempt to form such an opinion in all the historical light now avilable, is an education to the mind, the sort of education we all most terribly need.”
‘But, if we calmly study the past from as many angles as possible, we shall all of us gin in wisdom and understanding. We shall acquire a mentality which, when we return to our own problems, will be less at the mercy of newspapers and films. trying to make us take short cuts to truth and to oversimplify the tangled skein of human affairs.”
- G.M. Trevelyan: An autobiography and other essays.

patippuri
‘varalāṟu eṉpatu uṇarccikaḷiṉ tokuppu alla. uṇmi nikaḻvukaḷiṉ tokuppu.’ ‘varalāṟu eṉpatu ōr iṉattiṉ vāḻvukkum tāḻvukkum cāṉṟu kūṟum niṉivuc ciṉṉam’. ‘varalāṟu eṉpatu mānta iṉattiṉ aṟivukkaruvūlam’. ‘tamiḻiṉattiṉ varalāṟu neṭiyatu; nīṇṭatu; paḻamperumi mikkatu; āyvulakiṟku ariya pāṭaṅkaḷit taruvatu.’ varalāṟu eṉpatu kaṭanta kālattiṉ nikaḻvukaḷiyum, vāḻum kālattiṉ nikaḻvukaḷiyum, etirkālattiṉ nikaḻvukaḷiyum uyirppākak kāṭṭuvatu.
‘varalāṟu eṉpatu eṉṉa? muṟkālattil eṉṉa naṭantirukkalām eṉpatippaṟṟi pintiya talimuṟiyic cārnta māntaṉoruvaṉ taṉ maṉattil uruvākkik koḷvatē varalāṟākum eṉpar kāri pekmaṉ (Gary Bachman: JAOS - 2005) . ivvaralāṟṟi eḻutupavar aṟivunili, maṉanili, camutāyap pārvi,paṭṭaṟivu, kaṇṇōṭṭam, piṟacāṉṟukaḷi muṉvittu eḻutuvar. aḵtu aṟiviyal, kali, camayam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ūṟṟākat tikaḻvatu.
ōr iṉattiṉ vāḻvum tāḻvum anta iṉam naṭantu vanta kālaṭiccuvaṭukaḷip poṟuttē amiyum. oru nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟiṟku an nāṭṭil eḻunta ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭuḷḷa kalveṭṭukkaḷ, putiporuḷkaḷ, ceppēṭukaḷ, nāṇayaṅkaḷ, paḻaṅkālak kaṭṭaṭac citivukaḷ, ciṟpac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ, camayaṅkaḷ tāṉ cāṉṟu pakarvaṉa. attaṉiyum perumaḷavu uḷḷa maṇ tamiḻ maṇṇē ākum. moḻi, iṉa, nāṭṭu varalāṟu eṉpatu oru kuṭumpattiṟku eḻutappaṭum āvaṇam pōṉṟatu. ivvaralāṟu uṇmi varalāṟāka amiya vēṇṭum eṉpār moḻinūl mūtaṟiñar pāvāṇar.
intiyap perunilattiṉ varalāṟu vaṭakkiliruntu eḻutappaṭak kūṭātu. anta varalāṟu intiyāviṉ teṉmuṉiyiliruntu eḻutap paṭavēṇṭum. atuvē uṇmi varalāṟāka amiyum eṉpatu maṉōṇmaṇīyam cuntaraṉār kūṟiyatu; viṉceṉṭ cumit ēṟṟatu.
tamiḻiṉattiṉ paḻamiyip pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷ piṉṉōkkip pārttu, tolkāppiyam uṭpaṭṭa caṅkanūlkaḷ aṉittiyum muḻumiyākac cevvaṉē payaṉpaṭutti āṅkilattil mutaṉ
mutalil 1929il tamiḻar varalāṟu eḻutiyavar pi.ṭi.cīṉivācayyaṅkār āvār. annūlil tamiḻarē teṉṉāṭṭil, ēṉ intiyāvil, toṉṟutoṭṭu vāḻntu varum maṇṇiṉ mintarkaḷ eṉpatit tam āḻnta āypula aṟivāl niṟuviṉār. tamiḻnāṭṭil iṉṟu varalāṟu, moḻiyiyal pōṉṟa tuṟikaḷil vallunarākat tikaḻum aṟiñar pi. irāmanātaṉ avarkaḷ annūliṉi tamiḻākkam ceytuḷḷār. ataṉit tamiḻar varalāṟu (ki.pi.600 vari) eṉum nūlākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam 2008il veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. ivvaralāṟṟu varicikkum tiru. irāmanātaṉ avarkaḷ aṟimukavurikaḷ vaḻaṅki aṇicērttuḷḷār.
pāṇṭiyar varalāṟu, piṟkālac cōḻarcarittiram, pallavar varalāṟu, kaḷappirar kālat tamiḻakam (varalāṟṟāciriyar nālvar nūlkaḷiṉ tokuppu), koṅku nāṭṭu varalāṟu, tuḷu nāṭṭu varalāṟu, tamiḻaka varalāṟu eṉum nūlkaḷit tēṭi eṭuttu tamiḻaka varalāṟṟukkup perumi cērkkum vakiyil tamiḻaka varalāṟṟu varici eṉum talippil veḷiyiṭukiṟōm. ivaṟṟuḷ perum pālāṉavi iṉṟum varalāṟṟu māṇavarkaḷum, varalāṟṟu āyvāḷarkaḷum paṭikka vēṇṭiya karuvinūlkaḷākavum, eṉṟum payaṉtarum (classic) nūlkaḷākavum karutappaṭavēṇṭiyavi. paḷḷikaḷ, kallūrikaḷ, palkalik kaḻaka varalāṟṟut tuṟikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ māṇavarkaḷum, kallūri/palkalikkaḻaka āyvuttuṟikaḷum innūlkaḷi vāṅkip payaṉkoḷvārāka. 
tamiḻaka varalāṟṟukku aṇikalaṉāka viḷaṅkum ivvaralāṟṟuk karuvūlaṅkaḷip paḻamikkum putumikkum perumi cērkkum vakiyil ti.vi.catācivappaṇṭārattār, oḷavi turicāmip
piḷḷi, mā.irācamāṇikkaṉār, ira,paṉṉīrcelvam, mayili cīṉi.vēṅkaṭacāmi, mu.aruṇācalam, pulavar kuḻanti, naṭaṉa.kācinātaṉ pōṉṟa perumakkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷaṉar. ivvarun tamiḻ perumakkaḷi naṉṟi uṇarvōṭu niṉivukūrkiṟōm. 
talimuṟi talimuṟiyākac collappaṭṭum kēṭkappaṭṭum vanta ariya ceytikaḷi tam aṟiviṉ āḻattāl viruppu veṟuppu kaḷukku appāṟpaṭṭu naṭunili niṉṟu uṇmi varalāṟāka eḻuti yuḷḷa in nūlkaḷ āyvulakiṟkum, tamiḻ ulakiṟkum peritum payaṉ paṭat takka ariya varalāṟṟup peṭṭakamākum. paṇṭit tamiḻkkulam naṭantu vanta pātiyi aṟintu koḷḷavum, iṉi naṭakkavēṇṭiya pāti itu eṉa aṟutiyiṭṭuk koḷḷavum, taḷḷa vēṇṭiyavi ivi, koḷḷavēṇṭiyavi ivi eṉat tiṭṭamiṭṭut tam etir kāla vāḻvukku vaḷamāṉavaṟṟi ākkik koḷḷavum, tamiḻ iṉattit tāṅki niṟkum maṇṇiṉ perumiyi uṇaravum, tolinōkkup pārviyuṭaṉ ivvaralāṟṟu variciyit tamiḻar muṉ tantuḷḷōm. vāṅkip payaṉ
koḷḷuṅkaḷ.

eṉum pāvēntar varikaḷi neñcil niṟuttuṅkaḷ. 
moḻiyālum, iṉattālum aṭimippaṭṭa varalāṟu tamiḻariṉ varalāṟu. ivvaralāṟṟi mīṭṭeṭukka uḻitta perumakkaḷi vaṇaṅkuvōm. avvakiyil uḻittu nam kaṇmuṉṉē vāḻntu maṟinta perumakkaḷuḷ taliciṟantōr tanti periyārum, moḻi ñāyiṟu pāvāṇarum āvar. ipperumakkaḷ aṉivariyum naṉṟi yuṇarvuṭaṉ inta nērattil vaṇaṅkuvōm. avarkaḷ vaḻi payaṇam toṭaruvōm.
tamiḻ iṉamē! eṉ tamiḻ iḷiyōrē! nam muṉṉōriṉ vāḻviyum tāḻviyum āḻa niṉiyuṅkaḷ. iṉattiṉ mīḷāt tuyari mīṭṭeṭukka muṉiyuṅkaḷ.

kaḷappirar kālat tamiḻaka varalāṟu












kaḷappirar kālat tamiḻakam 
(ki.pi. 3-6 nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ)
ira. paṉṉīr celvam (1973)

mukavuri
tamiḻaka varalāṟṟil caṅka kālattiṉ piṉṉar cumār 2, 3 nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷi iruṇṭakālam eṉṟa peyarilēyē varalāṟṟā ciriyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. ikkālap pakutiyil kaḷappirar eṉpōr tamiḻakattik kippaṟṟi āṇṭaṉar eṉṟa kuṟippukkaḷ pala kiṭitta pōtilum ivarkaḷ yār? eṅkiruntu, evvāṟu tamiḻakap pakutiyik kippaṟṟiṉar? eṉṟa pala ceytikaḷ teḷivākkap paṭavilli. ituvari kiṭittuḷḷa ceytikaḷiyum, ceyyappaṭṭu vantuḷḷa ārāyccikaḷiyum tuṇiyākak koṇṭu kaḷappirar varalāṟṟi viḷakkuvatē innūliṉ nōkkam.
caṅkam - caṅkakālam paṟṟiya ceytikaḷip pala tamiḻp pērāciriyarkaḷum, varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷum āyntu kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. kaḷappirar varalāṟṟip paṭikkumpōtu avarkaḷukku muṉpuḷḷa tamiḻakac ceytikaḷit terintu koḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉpataṟkāka, avarkaḷiṉ ārāyccik karuttukkaḷic curukkamāka muṟpakuti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itil kūṟappaṭum ceytikaḷ cila tirumpat tirumpac collap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi antatanta iṭaṅkaḷukku ēṟpa amikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ataṉāl avi kūṟiyatu kūṟal eṉṟa kuṟṟattiṉ pāṟ paṭumeṉak koḷḷātu avvappakutikaḷi viḷakkum karuviyākak koḷka.
cumār nāṉku āṇṭukaḷ mutukali vakuppu māṇavar
kaḷukku varalāṟṟup pāṭam colli varumpōtu avarkaḷ eḻuppiya viṉākkaḷum, naṭattiya vivātaṅkaḷum, ivarkaḷip paṟṟi viḷakka mākak kūṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟa avarkaḷ tam viruppamum innūl veḷivaruvataṟku aṭiniliyāka amintaṉa.
avarkaḷ tam viṉākkaḷukku viṭiyākavum, avarkaḷ tam viruppattiṉ niṟivākavum uṇmi kāṇavēṇṭum eṉṟa eṉatu uḷḷattiṉ ātma tiruptiyākavum innūl amintatu eṉṟāl mikiyākātu. ataṉāl, avarkaḷukku nāṉ mikavum naṉṟi celutta vēṇṭiya niliyiluḷḷēṉ.
tamiḻakattiṉ iruṇṭa kālac cūḻnili eṉap pallōrālum viṭṭu viṭappaṭṭa oru varalāṟṟiṉi aṟintu koḷvataṟku innūlil kāṇum ceytikaḷ ētāvatu oru vakiyil tuṇi ceyyumāṉāl atuvē innūl eḻutiyataṉ payaṉākum.
itaṉip paṭikkum naṇparkaḷ, tiṟaṉāyvāḷarkaḷ innūlil kāṇum muṉṉukkup muṉ muraṇāṉa ceytikaḷic cuṭṭik kāṭṭip putiya ceytikaḷ, viḷakkaṅkaḷ iruntāl kūṟuvārkaḷāyiṉ avaṟṟi makiḻvuṭaṉ ēṟṟu vēṇṭiya iṭaṅkaḷil cērttu innūliṉi mēlum ceppamuṭiyatākki aṭutta patippukkaḷi veḷiyiṭa muyalvēṉ.
innūl veḷivarak kāraṇamāka irunta naṇparkaḷ, māṇavarkaḷ aṉivarukkum nāṉ naṉṟik kaṭappāṭuṭiyēṉ. itaṉ accut tiruttaṅkaḷi uṭaṉiruntu caripārttuk koṭutta celvi ē.vi.jōcapiṉ ṭōratikkum, iti naṉmuṟiyil veḷiyiṭṭa māṇikkam kampeṉi nūl-veḷiyīṭṭāḷarkaḷukkum eṉatu naṉṟiyit terivittuk koḷkiṟēṉ.
ceṉṉi
2.10.1972 ira.paṉṉīrcelvam

kaṭiccaṅkam aḻintatu
ilakkiyam, ilakkaṇam, iyal, ici, nāṭakam mutalāṉa ariya nūṟkaḷip paṟṟit toṭarntu naṭantu vanta ārāycciyum, caṅkamum evvāṟu aḻintaṉa? tirukkuṟaḷ araṅkēṟiyataṉ viḷivākac caṅkam aḻintatu eṉṟum, kaṇṇaki maturiyit tīkkiriyākkiya piṉ caṅkattip purappōr yārumilli eṉṟum, kaḷappirar iṭi yīṭṭiṉāl caṅkac ceytikaḷ aṟiya muṭiyātu pōyviṭṭaṉa eṉṟum pala vēṟupaṭṭa karuttukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. āyiṉum, caṅkam aḻintatu eṉṟu karutuvatiṉum, aracaṉ valiyaṟṟavaṉāka āṭci ciṟappuṟa naṭakka vaḻiyilli, attuṭaṉ toṭarum pulavar tamiḻārāycciyum, toṭarntu naṭattum muyaṟciyum illāmal pōyviṭṭatu eṉṟu karutuvatē ēṟpuṭiyatākum. ataṟkāka cūḻnilikaḷ eṉṉa eṉpati īṇṭu nōkkuvōm.
kaṭic caṅkattiṉ iṟutiyil āṇṭa aracaṉ allatu kaṭic caṅkattip puranta aracaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ ukkirap peruvaḻuti. itu iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal kūṟum ceyti. avaṉ kālattiltāṉ pāṇṭiya nāṭu paṉṉiraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷ vaṟumiyiṉāl vāṭiyatu. aracaṉ pulavarkaḷi viḷittut tāṅkaḷē taṅkaḷukku vēṇṭiya iṭam ceṉṟu vāḻac colli aṉuppukiṉṟāṉ.
inta vaṟumi nili iruntamikku ilakkiyaṅkaḷēyaṉṟip piṟkālattil tōṉṟiya kalveṭṭuc cācaṉam oṉṟum cāṉṟāka uḷḷatu. atil cila purāṇac ceytikaḷ iruntālum varalāṟṟup pakuti iṉṟiyamiyātatu. itil, aracaṉ tēvakaṉṉiyar makkaḷik koṇarntu, avarkaḷik koṇṭu kēṇikaḷ veṭṭi, nāṭṭiṉi vaḷamākki vaṟumiyip pōkkiṉāṉ eṉṟa ceytiyik kāṇalām. kalveṭṭup pakutiyi nōkkuka,
‘ 	vicuvāvacu vikāci mī(u) yacu
tiṅkaṭkiḻamiyum uttarāṭamum
peṟṟanāḷ tēvēntira kuṭumpaṉ palāttuppaṭi:
muṉ tuvāpara yukattil ukkirap peruvaḻutiyum cōḻaṉum, cēraṉum ulakam vaṟumippaṭṭu irukkiṉṟa kālattilē tēvēntiraṉ pakkal maḻi kēṭkap pōṉaviṭattilē pakavāṉum maṉam makiḻntu ‘irum’ eṉṉa, cēraṉum, cōḻaṉum vaṇaṅkiyirukka, tēvēntiraṉum varici ceytāṟpōla kavaṭu niṉikka, pāṇṭiyaṉ kōpittu eḻuntu tēvakaṉṉikikaḷ makkaḷ nālu kuṭumpattārik kippiṭiyākak piṭittuk koṇṭu ceṉṉel vitiyum, kaṉṉal vitiyum, (katali) vitiyum, paṉi vitiyum mutalāṉa pala vittum, oru riṣapamum oru cāviyum koṇṭu pūmiyil vantāṉ. nālvaril mutalvaṉukkut tēvēntirak kuṭumpap paṭṭamum kaṭṭi mūṉṟu pērukku vāriyaṉ, akkacāli, (iḷantāriyaṉ) eṉṟu varicip paṭṭamum kaṭṭi, oru nāḷiyilē 1200 kiṇaṟu veṭṭi vēḷāṇmi kaṇṭapaṭiyiṉālē,....
(teṉṉintiyak kōyil cācaṉaṅkaḷ) 
pākam II eṇ.8637
eṉṟu antak kalveṭṭup pakuti amintuḷḷatu. ik kalveṭṭu tirunelvēli jillā caṅkaranāyiṉār kōvil kōpuravācal uṭpuṟam kīḻp pakkattiluḷḷatu. paḻiya tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇat tilum perumpaṟṟap puliyūr nampikaḷ inta vaṟumiyiṉi āṅkāṅkuk kuṟittuc cellukiṟār. 12:3, 44:30. ilīlā caṅkiraka attiyāyam 44il ivvaṟumi kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atil mūvēntarum intiraṉi nāṭi maḻi vēṇṭic celkiṉṟa ceyti kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
eṉavē, ukkirap peruvaḻuti kālattil nāṭṭil vaṟuminili mikuntiruntatu eṉpatum, ataṉāl makkaḷ peritum tuṉpuṟṟaṉar eṉpatum teḷivākiṟatu itiyē iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyaluriyilum kaṇṭōm.
ivvāṟu koṭiya vaṟumiyil vāṭip paṉṉiraṇṭāṇṭukaḷ tamiḻakattiṉ palvēṟiṭaṅkaḷiyum aṭikkalamākac ceṉṟaṭinta pulavarkaḷ, avvaṟumi nīṅkiya piṉ mīḷkiṉṟaṉar. atukālip pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉ avarkaḷ evvāṟu akkālap pakutiyil vāḻntārkaḷ eṉṟa ceytiyiṉi aṟiya vēṇṭiyavaṉāka, avar kaḷum ayintaṉ eṉpāṉ taṅkaḷik kāttaruḷiyatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉip piṉvarum taṉippāṭal oṉṟāl aṟiyalām.
“ 	kāli ñāyiṟu kaṭuṅkatir parappi
vēliyum kuḷaṉum veṭipaṭac cuvaṟit
tantiyi makkaḷ mukam pārāmal
venta cākam vevvē ṟaruttik
kuṇamuḷa taṉiyum koṭuttu vāḻnta
kaṇavaṉi makaḷir kaṇpā rāmal
viḻitta viḻiyellām vēṟṟuviḻi yāki
aṟavuri yiṉṟi maṟavuri peruki
urimaṟan toḻinta vūḻik kālattil
tāyillā tavarkkut tāyē yākavum
tantiyil lātavarkkut tantiyē yākavum
inta ñālat tiṭukkaṇ tīra
vantu tōṉṟiṉaṉ mānitik kiḻavaṉ
nīlañ cēru neṭumāl pōlvāṉ
ālañ cēri ayintaṉ eṉpāṉ
taṉkuṟi collāṉ piṟarpaḻi yuriyāṉ
maṟantum poyyāṉ vāymiyum kuṉṟāṉ
iṟantu pōkātu emmik kāttāṉ
varuntal vēṇṭām vaḻuti
iruntaṉam iruntaṉam iṭarkeṭut taṉaṉē.’
(taṉippāṭal tiraṭṭu pākam I pāṭal 9)
āyiṉum, ettaṉi pulavarkaḷ mīṇṭu vantirukka muṭiyum? vaṟumiyuṟṟalinta pulavarkaḷil cilar mēṟkūṟiya ayintaṉāl kāppāṟṟappaṭṭālum ettaṉiyō pēr āṅkāṅkut taṅkiyiruttal tāṉ iyalpu. oru cilarē maturikku mīṇṭu vantirukka muṭiyum. mēlum, muccaṅka varalāṟṟi nōkkumpōtu caṅkattukkuc caṅkam pulavartam eṇṇikki kuṟintu koṇṭutāṉ vantirukkiṟatu. kaṭic caṅkattil ārāyntavarkaḷ 49 pērtāṉē.
iṉi, avvāṟu mīṇṭuvanta cilaril aṉivarumē ciṟanta pulamiyuṭiyavarkaḷāka iruntiruppārkaḷ eṉṟum nām eṇṇa iṭamilli. orucilarē naṟpulami vāyntavarkaḷāka iruntirukka muṭiyum. ēṉ? aracaṉ poruḷ ilakkaṇam vallārik kāṇēṉ eṉṟu varuntiyirukkiṉṟa niliyil akkuṟiyit tīrttu vikkak kūṭiya aḷavukkup pulami peṟṟavarkaḷ illi eṉpatik kallāṭac ceyyuṭ pakutiyāl teḷivāka uṇaralām. 
‘ 	ulakiyal niṟuttum poruḷ marapu oṭuṅka
māṟaṉum pulavarum mayaṅkuṟu kāli
muntuṟum perumaṟi muḷittu aruḷvākkāl
aṉpiṉ aintiṇi eṉṟu aṟupatu cūttiram
kaṭalamutu eṭuttuk kariyil vittatupōl
parappiṉ iṉ tamiḻc cuvitiraṭṭi maṟṟavarkkut
teḷitarak koṭutta teṉtamiḻk kaṭavuḷ’
kaṭavuḷē nūl ceytu tantāṉ eṉpatu paṟṟiya vivātatti viṭuttu ārāyiṉum, iṟivaṉ aḷitta akapporuḷ nūlukku muṉ poruḷ ilakkaṇam kūṟum nūl illātu pōyiṟṟā? tolkāppiyap poruḷatikāratti avarkaḷ aṟiyātu pōyiṉarā? eṉṟa pala viṉākkaḷ eḻukiṉṟaṉa.
mēlum, iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyalukku uri kūṟat teriyāmal uḷḷam aḻikiṉṟaṉar avippulavarkaḷ. poruḷ kāṇa iṟivaṉiyē nāṭukiṉṟaṉar. piṉṉar ovvoruvarāka muyaṟci ceykiṉṟaṉar uri kūṟuvataṟku. iṟutiyil uruttira caṉmaṉik koṇṭu nalla uriyiṉit tērkiṉṟaṉar eṉṟa katiyellām eḻukiṉṟaṉa.
eṉavē, paṉṉiraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷ vaṟumiyil vāṭikkaḷittut tirumpiya pulavarkaḷ paḻiya ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷukku ēṟṟa urip poruḷ kāṇum tiṟaṉaṟṟup pōyviṭṭaṉar. uriyiṉ tēviyiṉip paṟṟip paḻam pāṭal oṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭati muṉṉark kaṇṭōm. vaṟkaṭattiṉ piṉṉar mīṇṭu vantuṟṟa pulavarkaḷ ilakkaṇa, ilakkiya aṟivu, nirampap peṟṟavarkaḷ allar eṉpatiyē icceytikaḷāl aṟikiṟōm. āka, nalla tamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷic caṅkam iḻantu viṭṭatu. pulavar tam aṭimoḻi kaḷik koṇṭu, piṉṉar avarkaḷ tattam karumaṅkaḷiyē talik koṇṭaṉar eṉpatiyum, pāṭal ceytalip poḻutu pōkkākavē koṇṭaṉar eṉpatiyum nām aṉumāṉikka muṭiyum.
ataṉāl, vaṟumiyiṉ piṉ caṅkam kūṭiyatāka aṟiya utavum nikaḻcci iṟiyaṉār akap poruḷukku urikāṇakkūṭiya pulavar kūṭṭam oṉṟē. atiltāṉ akapporuḷum kāṇap paṭukiṟatu. ataṟku uriyum vakukkappaṭukiṟatu. nūlum uriyum vaṟkaṭattiṉa piṉṉar orē kālattil tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉpiṉ caṅkattiṉ ceyti kaḷi uṇarutaṟku ēṟṟa ātāraṅkaḷ illi.
piṉṉar naṉmāṟaṉatu āṭci tuvaṅkukiṟatu. itaṉi nakkīrar avaṉip pāṭiya pāṭalāl aṟiyalām.(puṟanāṉūṟu:56) ivaṉ cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ pattiṉic cilikkuk kalleṭukka vaṭanāṭu ceṉṟu vaṭapulattaracarām kaṉaka viciyari veṉṟu avar tam taliyilē kalliyēṟṟik koṇarappōṉa kālattu, maturiyil kaṇṇaki vaḻakkurikka,
“ 	poṉcey kollaṉ taṉ col kēṭṭa
yāṉō aracaṉ? yāṉē kaḷvaṉ!
maṉpati kākkum teṉpulam kāval
eṉmutal piḻittatu keṭuka eṉ āyuḷ.’
(cilampu: vaḻakkuri kāti 74-76)
eṉakkūṟi iṟantupaṭṭa taṉ tantiyiṉ aracāṭciyiṉik kippaṟṟi muṭi cūṭukiṉṟāṉ. ivaṉ kālattilum vaṟumiyiṉ niḻal toṭarukiṉṟatu. ataṉāl kaṇṇaki vaḻipāṭṭil kalantu nāṭṭiṉic ceḻikkac ceytāṉ eṉpataṉi,
“aṉṟu toṭṭu, pāṇṭiyaṉ nāṭu maḻi vaṟam kūrntu, vaṟumi eyti, veppu nōyum kuruvum toṭara, koṟkiyil irunta veṟṟi vēṟceḻiyaṉ, naṅkikkup poṟkollar āyiravarik koṉṟu kaḷavēḷviyāl viḻavoṭu cānti ceyya nāṭu maliya maḻi peytu nōyum tuṉpamum nīṅkiyatu.”
eṉṟa cilappatikāra uri peṟu kaṭṭurip pakutiyāl aṟiyalām.
cācaṉaṅkaḷil pūtattuṇi koṇṭu kēṇi, kuḷaṅkaḷum veṭṭi nāṭṭi vaḷamākkiṉar pāṇṭiyar eṉṟu pārkkiṉṟōm. mēlē kāṭṭiya (teṉṉintiyak kōyil cācaṉaṅkaḷ pākam II, eṇ: 8637) kalveṭṭil tēvakaṉṉiyar kuṭumpattil nālvarik koṇṭu vantu paṇittu nāṭṭik kāttaṉar eṉṟu pārttōm. paḻiya tiruviḷi yāṭalil mēka maṉṉar nālvarip piṭittup paṇikoṇṭu nāṭṭic ceḻikkac ceytaṉar eṉṟu pārkkiṉṟōm. iṅku naṉmāṟaṉ kaṇṇaki vaḻipāṭṭil kalantu nāṭṭi vaḷappaṭutta muyalukiṉṟāṉ. āyiṉum, nāṭu paḻiya cāyaliliruntu viṭupaṭavilli. caṅkattip purakkavum vāyppilli. piyappiyac caṅkac ceytikaḷum nammiviṭṭu maṟiyalāyiṉa.


II. vēṟṟup pulattavar nuḻintaṉar
vaṟkaṭattāl makkaḷum maṉṉaṉum ceyaliḻantu vali kuṉṟiyirukkiṉṟa kālattil tāṉ kālam karutiyirunta vēṟṟup pulattavar, vaṟumik kālac cūḻniliyip payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭu eḻucci peṟṟup pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉik kulittu mēlōṅkiyirukka vēṇṭum. avarkaḷ avvāṟu eḻucci peṟat takkavāṟu taṅkaḷ celvākkiṉiyum ciṟitu ciṟitākap peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum. taṅkaḷiṉ eḻuccikku etirppukkaḷ tōṉṟā vaṇṇam nāṭṭil avarkaḷ nili peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum. avvāṟilliyāyiṉ iṉanteriyāta oru kūṭṭam tiṭīreṉa nuḻintu, veṉṟu āṭciyik kippaṟṟum aḷavukkuc celvākkup peṟa muṭiyātu.
avvāṟu vēṟṟup pulattavar tamiḻakattiṉ teṉ pakutiyāṉa pāṇṭiya nāṭṭik koḷḷi koḷḷum pōtu piṟatamiḻ aracarkaḷ eṉṉa ceytu koṇṭiruntārkaḷ? eṅkiruntārkaḷ? avarkaḷ vēṟṟup pulattāriṉ taliyīṭṭiṉit taṭukka muṟpaṭavilliyā? allatu avarkaḷ pāṇṭiya nāṭu aḻivupaṭum muṉpēyē aṭimikaḷākic curuṅki viṭṭiruntaṉarā? eṉṟa kēḷvikaḷ ellām ārāyvataṟkuriyaṉavē.
nuḻintavar kaḷappirar
avvāṟu kālam karutiyirunta vēṟṟup pulattavar yār? pāṇṭiyariṉ paḻamperum cācaṉamāṉa vēḷvik kuṭic ceppēṭṭuc cācaṉam itaṟku viṭiyiṟukkum mutal mūlamākum. (iccācaṉam nūl vaṭivil muḻu vaṭivil cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷatu) accācaṉattiṉ muṉ pakutiyiṉ cāram piṉvarumāṟu:
pal yākacāli mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti eṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ, cila pārppaṉarkaḷukku vēḷvit tāṉamākap pākaṉūrk kūṟṟattuṭpaṭum cila pakutikaḷi vēḷvikkuṭi eṉṟa peyaril nīr vārttut tāṉamākak koṭukkiṉṟāṉ. avaṉukkup piṉṉar aḷavaṟṟa pala aracarkaḷiṉ āṭci naṭakkiṉṟatu. piṉṉar kaḷappiraṉ eṉṟa oru kali aracaṉ nāṭṭik kippaṟṟi, anta nivantattiyum paṟimutal ceytu viṭukiṉṟāṉ. ataṉpiṉ cila āṇṭukaḷ kaḻikiṉṟaṉa. kaḷappira vamcattavariṉ āṭciyākiya paṉiyiṉi nīkkum pakalavaṉ pōlat tōṉṟiya kaṭuṅkōṉ eṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ kaḷappirari aḻittu mīṇṭum pāṇṭiyap pēraraciṉi nili nāṭṭukiṉṟāṉ. vēḷvik
kuṭic ceppēṭukaḷil,
“ vēntaṉ appoḻutēy nīrōṭu aṭṭik koṭuttamiyāl
nīṭu pukti tuyttapiṉ aḷavariya ātirājari akalanīkki
akaliṭattik kaḷapraṉ eṉṉum kaliyaricaṉ kikkoṇṭu
ataṉi iṟakkiyapiṉ,
paṭukaṭaṉ muḷitta paruti pōla pāṇṭiyāti rājaṉ veḷiṟpaṭṭu
oṭuṅkā maṉṉar oḷinakar aḻitta
kaṭuṅkōṉ eṉṉum katirvēl teṉṉaṉ’.
eṉṟa pakutiyum
	palyāka mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti eṉṉum paramēcuvaraṉāl
vēḷvikuṭi eṉappaṭṭatu; kēḷviyil tarappaṭṭataṉi
tuḷakkam illāk kaṭal tāṉiyāy
kaḷappirarāl iṟakkappaṭṭatu.’
eṉṟa pakutiyum icceytikaḷi naṉku teḷivākkum.
eṉavē, palyākacāli mutukuṭumip peruvaḻutikkup piṉṉar nīṇṭa kālam attāṉatti aṉupavikkiṉṟaṉar avvantaṇarkaḷ. pala vēṟu aracarkaḷ pāṇṭiya āṭciyi ēṟṟu naṭattiyirukka vēṇṭum. iṟutiyil valiyaṟṟa oruvaṉatu kālattil kaḷappirar aḷavariya ātirācari akala nīkki nāṭṭik kippaṟṟiyirukka vēṇṭum. iṅku ātirācar eṉṟa paṉmikkup pala aracarkaḷ āṇṭa āṭciyiṉi nīkki eṉṟē koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
ivvāṟākak kaḷappirarkaḷ nuḻintu piramatēyaṅkaḷik kik koṇṭakkāli mīṇṭum eḻucci peṟṟu vantirukkiṉṟa pāṇṭiya kulattil kaṭuṅkōṉ eṉpavaṉ tōṉṟik kaḷappirar āṭciyi aḻittu mīṇṭum pāṇṭiyap pēraraciṉi niṟuvukiṉṟāṉ.
‘kaḷaprarik kaḷikaṭṭa māk kaṭuṅkōṉ’ eṉṟum ‘‘kaḷappāḻar kulam kaḷintum’ eṉṟum varukiṉṟa piṟkālac cācaṉat toṭar kaḷilum kaṭuṅkōṉ kaḷappirari veṉṟāṉ eṉṟa ceytiyik kāṇalām.
eṉavē, kaṭuṅkōṉ eṉṟa pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉukku muṉṉar āṭci ceytavarkaḷ tamiḻ maṉṉarkaḷ allar eṉṟa ceytiyum, avarkaḷ antaṇarkaḷukkuk koṭukkappaṭṭa tāṉaṅkaḷip paṟittuk koṇṭa vēṟṟuc camayattavar eṉpatum pulaṉām.
ivvāṟāṉa araciyal kuḻappattiṉ kāraṇamākap paṇṭu koṭukkappaṭṭa tāṉamāṉatu, kaḷappirar āṭci oḻinta piṉṉarum, pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñcaṭiyaṉ kālam vari yārālum kavaṉikkap paṭāmal allatu uṇarappaṭāmal iruntirukkiṉṟatu. ki.pi.8ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil tāṉ muṉpu tāṉam peṟṟa koṟkik kiḻāṉ naṟkoṟṟaṉ eṉpavaṉiṉ vaḻivantavaṉākiya koṟkik kiḻāṉ cuvaraṉ ciṅkaṉ eṉpavaṉ ippaḻiya tāṉam paṟṟi aracaṉiṭam ceṉṟu muṟiyiṭukiṟāṉ. itu cācaṉam kūṟum ceyti. ivaṟṟi yellām koṇṭu pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil tamiḻ vēntar āṭcikkuppiṉ nuḻintavar kaḷappirar eṉpatit teḷiyalām.


III. kaḷappirar yār
kaḷappirar tāyakam
nuḻintavar kaḷappirar eṉiṉ, ivarkaḷ evvāṟu pāṇṭiya nāṭṭukkuḷ nuḻintaṉar? eṅkiruntu vantaṉar? araca kuṭiyiṉarā? eṉṟa pala viṉākkaḷ eḻukiṉṟaṉa. ceppēṭukaḷil ‘kali aracaṉ’ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. āṉāl, kali aracaṉ eṉpataṟkuk koṭiya aracaṉ eṉṟa viḷakkattiyē palarum kūṟip pōntaṉar. ivarkaḷ ōr araca kuṭumpattic cārntavarkaḷāka irukkalām eṉṟu tiru kiruṣṇa cāstiri karutukiṉṟār. cāḷukkiya maṉṉaṉ iraṇṭām pulikēciyiṉ kopparam ceppēṭukaḷ ‘kalikulam’ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa.
mēlum, vaṭa nāṭṭil ‘kalki aptam’ eṉṟa oru āṇṭu muṟiyiṉic camaṇa camaya aṭippaṭiyil taḻuvukiṉṟa kālattut teṉ pakutiyil ‘kaliyaptam’ eṉṟa oru āṇṭu muṟiyiṉip putta camaya aṭippaṭiyil ēṟpaṭuttiṉar eṉṟa karuttiṉiyum iṅku nām kuṟippiṭal vēṇṭum.
kutama puttaratu ciliyiṉic cāmuṇṭarāyaṉ eṉpavaṉ ki.pi.4ām nūṟṟāṇṭil nilipeṟac ceytāṉ eṉṟa ceytiyik kūṟum kalveṭṭu kaliyaptam 983il veṭṭappaṭṭatu eṉṟu āyvāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
mēlum, kali, kaliya eṉṟa coṟkaḷiṉ payiṟci perumpālum kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭil mikuti. kali tēva vaṇakkam iruttaliyum appeyar koṇṭa tēvaṉukkut tāṉaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya ceyti kaḷiyum annāṭṭuk kalveṭṭukkaḷiliruntu aṟiyalām.
eṉavē, kali tēvaṉ toṭarpāl ēṟpaṭṭa oru kulam kalikulam eṉpatum, avarkaḷ kaliyaptam eṉṟa oru āṇṭu muṟiyiṉik koṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpatum, avarkaḷ pūrvīkam tamiḻakattiṉa vaṭa ellikku appāl kaṉṉaṭattiṉ teṉ pakutiyākum eṉpatum pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa.
āyiṉ, kalikkum kaḷappirarukkum uḷḷa toṭarpu eṉṉa? kaliyaracaṉ kaḷapraṉ eṉṟu koḷḷiṉum ‘kaḷaprarāl iṟakkappaṭṭatu’ eṉṟa toṭar kaḷappirar eṉṟa oru iṉattik kuṟippiṭuvatāka uḷḷatē aṉṟit taṉitta oru aracaṉik kuṟippatākat tōṉṟa villi. eṉavē, kalikulam eṉpatum, kaḷaprar kulam eṉpatum oṉṟō vēṟō eṉṟa aiyamum eḻukiṉṟatu.
iṉi, ciravaṇapalkōlā maliyil uḷḷa kalveṭṭu oṉṟil ‘kaḷavappu allatu kaḷapappu’ eṉṟa col payiṉṟu vantuḷḷatu. ataṉ vaṭamoḻi mūlam ‘kaḷavapra’ eṉpatu. pēlūrp pakutiyil vāḻum oru paḻaṅkuṭikkuk ‘kaḷapōrā’ eṉṟa peyar iruppatāka oru paḻiya kaṉṉaṭak kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.
ki.pi.5ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyil vāḻnta katampa kulattaracaṉ kākuttaṉukkuk ‘kaḷapōrar’ pakiyāka iruntārkaḷ. hōskeṭe vīrakkal cācaṉam ‘kaḷavara nāṭu’ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭu
kiṉṟatu. oru aracaṉukkup pakivaṉāka irukkum niliyi nōkkum pōtum, kaḷavara nāṭu eṉṟu oru nilap pakutiyik kuṟikkum pōtum avarkaḷi oru nāṭṭiṉi āḷum takutipeṟṟa araca kuṭiyiṉarākat tāṉ karuta muṭiyum.
kaḷapappu nāṭṭi āṭci ceyta tiṇṭīkaṉ mēlik kaṅkaṉuṭaṉ uṟavukoṇṭu pala tāṉaṅkaḷic ceytāṉ eṉṟu pārkkiṉṟōm. ivaṉ ki.pi. 3, 4 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iruntavaṉ. it tiṇṭīkaṉ mēlik kaṅkar āṭciyil kiruṣṇavarma makātirāyaṉukkup piṉ, araca kumārar iṉmiyāl av vamcattit taṉ pālatākkik koṇṭu āṭci ceytavaṉ eṉṟa ceytiyik ‘koṅku tēca rācākkaḷ caritam’ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
eṉavē, teṉ kaṉṉaṭap pakutiyil ivarkaḷ vāḻntavar
kaḷātal vēṇṭum eṉṟum, araca kuṭi niliyil irunta mutukuṭi yiṉarāka malip pakutiyil paraviyiruntirukka vēṇṭum allatu katamparkaḷāl viraṭṭappaṭṭu teṉ kaṉṉaṭap pakutiyil pukuntu nili peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟum aṟiyalām. mēlum,
‘ 	perumpukaḻ accutak kōvē!
nantimā mali cilampa!
nanti niṟparavutal nāvalark karitē!
eṉṟu varukiṉṟa ceyyuṭpakuti ivarkaḷ nantimalip pakutikkut talivarkaḷāka iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟa ceytiyi uṟutip paṭuttukiṉṟatu. ataṟkut talivaṉāka iruntavaṉ ‘accutaṉ’ eṉpatiyum aṟikiṟōm. itu piṉṉar viḷakkappaṭum.
eṉavē, kaḷappirarkaḷ teṉ kaṉṉaṭap pakutiyiṉit tāyakamākak koṇṭu malivāḻ araca kuṭi makkaḷākat taṉitta āṭciyamippil vāḻntavarkaḷāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. āyiṉum, katamparkaḷiṉ vaṉmiyāl ivarkaḷ viraṭṭappaṭṭuk kōlār - nantimalip pakutikaḷil pukuntirukkiṉṟaṉar. ciravaṇa palkōlā camaṇa camayattukkup perum ātaravu aḷikkum iṭam, ātaliṉ, ivarkaḷum accamayattit taḻuviyiruttal vēṇṭum. āyiṉum, putta matattiyum ivarkaḷ ātarittupa pōṟṟiyuḷḷaṉar eṉpati accamaya nūlkaḷiṉ kuṟippukkaḷāl aṟiyalām.
tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil vaṭukar
mēl, tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ vaṭa ellikku appāl kaṉṉaṭap pakutiyil vāḻntavarkaḷātal vēṇṭum ik kaḷappirarkaḷ eṉṟu kaṇṭōm. tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil tamiḻaka vaṭa elliyāka vēṅkaṭamum, vēṅkaṭattiṉa appāl iruntatu moḻi peyar tēyam eṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
‘ 	vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉ kumari
āyiṭit
tamiḻkūṟu nallulakam’
(tolkāppiyam - ciṟappuppāyiram)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyamum,
“	vaṭāatu paṉipaṭu neṭuvari vaṭakkum
teṉāatu urukeḻu kumariyiṉ teṟkum
kuṇāatu kariporu toṭukaṭal kuṇakkum
kuṭāatu toṉṟu mutir puvattiṉ kuṭakkum”
eṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟu 6ām pāṭalum:
“	teṉkumari vaṭaperuṅkal
kuṇakuṭa kaṭalā velli
kuṉṟu mali kāṭu nāṭu
oṉṟu paṭṭu vaḻi moḻiya”
eṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟu 17ām pāṭalum;
“ teṉkumari vaṭaperuṅkal
kuṇakuṭa kaṭalāvellit
toṉṟu moḻintu toḻil kēṭpa” 
(maturik kāñci : 70-72)
eṉṟu maturik kāñciyum tamiḻakattiṉ teṟku, vaṭakku, kiḻakku, mēṟku ākiya ticikaḷukkuriya ellikaḷi viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa.
“ ....vaṭukar muṉiyatu
valvēṟ kaṭṭi naṉṉāṭṭumpar
moḻi peyar tēem” (kuṟuntoki: 11)
eṉṟu vēṅkaṭattiṉ appāṟpaṭṭa pakutikaḷ moḻipeyar tēyam eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭatik kuṟuntokiyil māmūlaṉārkuṟittuḷḷār.
eṉavē, tamiḻ moḻiyallāta vēṟṟu moḻi pēcum makkaḷiyuṭiyatu annāṭu eṉpatiyum, avvāṟu avarkaḷ vēṟṟu moḻiyiṉarāyiṉum tamiḻp pulavarkaḷi ātarittaṉar eṉpatiyum, pulavarkaḷ tamiḻakattiṉa vaṭa elliyik kuṟikkumpōtu vēṅkaṭamaliyik kūṟuvatōṭu appāl uḷḷatu moḻi peyar tēyam eṉṟum aṟintiruntaṉar eṉpatiyum teḷivāka uṇaralām.
anta moḻi peyar tēyatti āṇṭavaṉ ‘pulli’ eṉṟa aracaṉ eṉa ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kūṟum.
“ 	puṭiyalaṅ kaḻaṟkāṟ pulli kuṉṟattu
naṭiyaruṅ kāṉam vilaṅki nōṉcilit
toṭiyamipakaḻit tuvaṉṟunili vaṭukar
piḻiyār makiḻar kaliciṟan tārkkum
moḻipeyar tēem.” (akam: 295)
eṉṉum pāṭal itaṟkuk kāṭṭāka amintuḷḷatu. ippulliyik ‘kaḷvar kōmāṉ māvaṇ pulli’ eṉṟu pulavarkaḷ ciṟappit
tuḷḷaṉar. itaṉi
“ 	kaḻalpuṉi tiruntaṭik kaḷvar kōmāṉ
maḻapulam vaṇakkiya māvaṇ pulli
viḻavuṭiya viḻuccīr vēṅkaṭam” (akam:61)
eṉṉum māmūlaṉār pāṭalāl aṟiyalām. kaḷvar-- kaḷavar eṉpataṉ marūu; allatu kaḷvar eṉṟu puḷḷi koṭuttu eḻutiyuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu karutavum iṭamuṇṭu. kaḷvar nāṭu eṉpatu kaṉṉaṭak kalveṭṭuk
kaḷil ‘kaḷavara nāṭu’ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappeṟṟuḷḷatu. kaḷapra eṉpatu pakara vakarap parimāṟṟattāl ‘kaḷavar’ eṉat tirintu, tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil kaḷavar allutu kaḷvar eṉṟu eḻutap peṟalāyiṟṟu eṉak koḷḷalām. iṉik ‘kaḷvar’ eṉṟa tamiḻ ‘kaḷavar’ eṉṟum ‘kaḷapra’ eṉṟum tirintatu eṉa vātittiṭalum oṉṟu.
ivarkaḷatu peyaraṭikaḷik koṇṭum, ivarkaḷatu nāṭu vēṅkaṭamum, vēṅkaṭattumparum eṉpatu koṇṭum, atu tamiḻ moḻiyallāta vēṟṟu moḻi payilap peṟṟa moḻi peyar tēyam eṉpatu koṇṭum kaḷappirar pulliyiṉattavarāka vēṅkaṭamalic cāral pakutiyiṉi āṇṭu vanta oru malivāḻ aracakuṭiyiṉarāka iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟa muṭivukku varalām.
eṉavē, tiṭīreṉṟu oru marapiṉar tōṉṟi nāṭṭik kippaṟṟi āṇṭaṉar eṉṟu koḷvatiṉum, kaḷavar kulattup pulliyaracaṉ vaḻiyiṉar tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ cīr kēṭuṟṟa niliyiṉip payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭu atik kippaṟṟiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉavō, pallavar ātikkam vaṭapāl mēlōṅkiyatu kaḷavar kulattār nāṭu piṭikka vēṟṟiṭam nāṭic cellak kāraṇamāka iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉavō koḷḷalām. ākavē, toṇṭi nāṭṭil celvākkup peṟa muṭiyāmal tamiḻnāṭṭik kippaṟṟum eṇṇattuṭaṉ teṟku nōkki vantu cōṇāṭṭik kippaṟṟi avvaḻiyē pāṇṭiya nāṭṭukkuḷ pukuntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpati oruvāṟu teriyalām.
eṉiṉ, kaḷavarap pulliyiṉattaracarkaḷiṉ camaya nili eṉṉa? eṉṟa viṉā eḻukiṉṟatu caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷik koṇṭu ārāyu
miṭattu aṉṟiya vāḻvil makkaḷāl vaṇaṅkappaṭṭa allatu pōṟṟappaṭṭa kaṭavuḷarkaḷip paṟṟit terintu koḷḷalāmē aṉṟic camaya nilikaḷ - camaya tattuvaṅkaḷ - koḷkikaḷ mutalāṉa
vaṟṟit teḷivāka aṟintu koḷvatu eṉpatu uṇmiyil iyalāta oṉṟu. avvāṟāṉa oru kuṟippiṭṭa tattuvanili aṉṟu illātatāltāṉ putta - camaṇa camayaṅkaḷ eḷitiṉ nuḻintu celvākkup peṟa muṭintatu. avvāṟu celvākkup peṟṟa niliyil avi tattam kōṭpāṭukaḷip parappa avarkaḷ moḻiyiṉiyē payaṉpaṭuttiyiruttal vēṇṭum. eṉavē, moḻi peyar tēyattuk kaḷavar talivaṉ pulliyum avaṉ iṉattārum tamiḻp pulavarkaḷi ātarittaṉar eṉpatu oru puṟamirukka, tam nāṭṭu moḻiyiṉiyum, camayattiṉiyum, camayac celvākkiṉāl iṭam peṟṟa pākatam, pāli moḻikaḷiyum pōṟṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatum orutali.
ippulliyiṉ peyaruṭaṉ ōrūr kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭil iruntatiyum, aṅku arukak kaṭavuḷ kōyil iruntatatiyum, aṅku arukak kaṭavuḷ kōyil iruntatiyum kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭu hosak kōṭṭic ceppēṭukaḷ (pallavar ceppēṭukaḷ muppatu pak.378) kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa.
‘koṅkaṇiyatirācaṉatu paṉṉiraṇṭāvatu āṭciyāṇṭil, pallava cimma viṣṇuviṉ tāy pulliyūr eṉṟa kirāmattil eḻuntaruḷuvitta arukak kaṭavuḷiṉ kōyilukku nivantamāka avvūr ēriyiṉ kīḻppakutikaḷil nañci nilam, vīṭu mutalāṉavaṟṟi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟāḷ’ eṉṟu acceppēṭukaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. itil ‘pulliyūr’ eṉpatu ‘pulli’ eṉṟu tamiḻ ilakkiyap pāṭalkaḷ kuṟikkum aracaṉatu peyariṉ piṉ tōṉṟiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉpati nām tuṇiyalām.
itupōṉṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷāl ‘kaḷavar’ tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭa elliyil vāḻnta teṉṉintiya aracakuṭiyiṉarē eṉpatu pōtaru
kiṉṟatu. tamiḻakattiṉ vaṭa elliyil mēṟkiliruntu kiḻakkāka eṭuttuk koṇṭāl koṅkaṇar, katampar, kaḷavar, toṇṭiyarkaḷ āṇṭu vantirukkiṉṟaṉar eṉpatu teḷivu.
‘ .....teṉāatu
velpōrk kavuriyar taruppuḻi tiṟappiṉ
ākoḷ mūtūrk kaḷvar perumakaṉ
ēvaliḷiyar talivaṉ mēvār
aruṅkuṟum peṟinta āṟṟaloṭu paruntupaṭap
palceruk kaṭanta celluṟaḻ taṭakkik 
keṭāa nallicit teṉṉaṉ’ (akam: 342)
eṉṟa aka ilakkiyap pāṭal oṉṟil pāṇṭiyaṉi maturik kaṇakkāyaṉār ‘kaḷvar perumakaṉ’ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār. orōvaḻi ippāṇṭiyaṉ kaḷvar pulliyi veṉṟatāl ēṟpaṭṭa ciṟappu aṭimoḻiyākavum irukkalām.
kaḷappirarum muttariyarum
ki.pi. eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavil tañci, vallam, putukkōṭṭip pakutikaḷi muttariyarkaḷ eṉṟa oru iṉattavar āṇṭu vantaṉar. ivarkaḷ pallavarukkut tuṇiyākap pāṇṭiyaṉi etirttuca ceyta perumpōriṉi viḷakkum pāṭalkaḷ centaliyil uḷḷa kōyil tūṇkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi ‘kaḷvāra kaḷvaṉ’ eṉṟum, kaḷvar perumakaṉ eṉṟum ivarkaḷik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟāṉ. mēlum, vijayālaya cōḻaṉ ki.pi.9ām nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavil cōḻa nāṭṭi mīṇṭum kippaṟṟukiṉṟa kāli, immuttariyariṭamiruntutāṉ kippaṟṟiṉāṉ eṉṟu cōḻar kalveṭṭuc cācaṉaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉit ‘tañci koṇṭa kōpparakēcari’ eṉṟu vīracōḻapuram kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṉṟatu. ākavē, vijayālayaṉukku muṉpu tañci vēṟṟaracar kippiṭiyil iruntatu eṉpatiyum avarkaḷ muttariyarkaḷāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉpatiyum aṟiyalām.
eṉiṉ, muttariyar yār? avarkaḷukku ippeyar evvāṟu vantatu? mēl kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa kaḷavarkaḷ mūvēntarkaḷiyum veṉṟu oru kuṭikkīḻ nāṭṭi āṇṭaṉar eṉṟu pāṭalkaḷil kāṇappaṭu
kiṉṟatu. avvāṟu mūvēntariyum veṉṟu mūṉṟu nāṭṭiyum kippaṟṟiya ciṟappuk karuti ivarkaḷ muttariyarkaḷ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟa oru viḷakkattiṉiyum kūṟamuṭiyum.
ivviṭattil vaṭavaṭukar paṟṟit tamiḻilakkiyattil varukiṉṟa,
“ 	teṉparatavar miṭal cāya
vaṭavaṭukar vāḷōṭṭiya
toṭiyami kaṇṇit tiruntu vēṟṟaṭakkik
kaṭumākaṭiiya viṭupari vaṭimpiṉ
naṟṟārk kaḷḷiṉ cōḻaṉ kōyil
putup piṟiyaṉṉa cuti cey māṭam” (puṟam : 378)
eṉṟa pakuti nōkkaṟ pālatu.
‘perumpiṭuku’ ‘māṟpiṭuku’ eṉṟu aṭimoḻikaḷālum, ‘kāṭakka muttariyar’ eṉṟu varukiṉṟa toṭarāluma mutalil ivarkaḷ pallavariṉ kīḻ iruntaṉar eṉpatiyum, ‘vāḷvari vēṅki kuttiyatu’ eṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟa cācaṉat toṭarāl ivarkaḷ piṉṉar cōḻariṉ kīḻ iruntaṉar eṉpatiyum tuṇiya muṭiyum. ivvāṟu tam pēraracu nili taḷarntu, valikuṉṟi vēṟṟaracarkaḷiṉ kīḻ ciṟu talivarkaḷāka irunta kālattum ‘muttariyar’ eṉṟa ciṟappu aṭimoḻiyuṭaṉēyē vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu vantuḷḷaṉar.
‘māṟaṉ’ eṉṟu ivarkaḷic cācaṉaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. pallava-pāṇṭiyap pōril tuṇi ceṉṟu pāṇṭiyari veṟṟi koṇṭa kāraṇattāl, appeyari ivarkaḷukku iṭṭu vaḻaṅki yirukkiṉṟaṉar pāṭal āciriyarkaḷ.
iṉi, ivarkaḷ kaḷavar marapiṉar eṉpataṟku, ‘kaḷvar kaḷvaṉ’, ‘kaḷavāra kaḷvaṉ, eṉṟa toṭarkaḷum ‘muttariyar’ eṉṟa peyaruma, kalveṭṭukkaḷil oru cērap payilap peṟṟiruttalē cāṉṟākum.
kaṉṉaṭak kalveṭṭukkaḷil muttariyar
kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭup pakutikaḷi āṇṭuvanta kaṅka aracarkaḷukku ‘muttaracar’ eṉṟa ciṟappu aṭimoḻi iruntuḷḷatu. kaṅkaṉ srīpuruṣa makārācaṉi ivvāṟāṉa aṭimoḻiyuṭaṉ kuṟippiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷ kaṉṉaṭac cācaṉaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
“ svastisrī koṅkaṇi muttaraca srī puruṣa
 mahā rājahtirāja paramēsvara...” (HGOK:P:118, No:24)
“ svasti srīmat
 koṅkoṇi muttaraca
 r ayicatē....” (I Bid:P:124)
eṉa varukiṉṟa toṭarkaḷi nōkkumiṭattu, kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭi āṇṭa kaṅka aracarkaḷukku muttaracar eṉṟa ciṟappuṇmiyi aṟiyalām. ivarkaḷukkum katamparkaḷukkum araciyal pōrāṭṭam iruntuḷḷatu eṉpatic carittiram kūṟum. ivarkaḷ camaṇa camayac cārpumikkavarkaḷ eṉpatiyum camaṇa camayat tattuvaṅkaḷil tuṟi pōkiyavarkaḷ eṉpatiyum koṅkutēca rācākkaḷ eṉṟa carita nūl kūṟukiṉṟatu.
eṉavē, kaḷavara nāṭṭiṉarākiya kaṅkarkaḷ, kaḷappirar eṉṟum, muttariyar eṉṟum ciṟappuṭaṉ vāḻntu vantuḷḷaṉar eṉpatum, kaḷavara nāṭu micūrppakutiyil uḷḷatāl atuvē avarkaḷ tāyakam ākalām eṉpatum, kaḷavara nāṭu eṉtē tirintu ‘kaṉṉāṭu’ eṉṟum, ‘kaṉṉaṭa nāṭu’ eṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvatāyiṟṟu eṉpatum, atu vaṭukar nāṭu eṉṟu tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil paṇṭu kuṟippiṭappeṟṟuḷḷatu eṉpatum, atu tamiḻmoḻiyallāta moḻi peyar tēyam eṉpatum teḷivāka aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.
“ nērā vaṉtōḷ vaṭukar perumakaṉ
pērici yerumi naṉṉāṭ ṭuḷḷati
ayiriyā ṟiṟantaṉar....” (akam 253)
eṉṟa in nakkīrar pāṭalāl vaṭukar nāṭu micūr (erumi naṉṉāṭu) eṉpataṉit teḷiyalām.
ākavē, kaḷappirar eṉpār tamiḻakattiṉ vaṭellikku appāl vāḻnta vēṟṟu moḻiyiṉar eṉpatum, kalitēva vaṇakkam koṇṭu, camaṇa camayat toṭarpuṭaṉ nantimalip pakutiyiṉik kippaṟṟi vāḻnta aracakuṭi makkaḷē eṉpatum, kaḷavar iṉatta ‘pulli’ eṉṟa aracaṉ orōvaḻi ivvamicattiṉic cārntavaṉākal vēṇṭum eṉpatum, araciyal kuḻappamum, nāṭu piṭikkum eṇṇamum, ivarkaḷ teṟku nōkki varak kāraṇamāka iruntuḷḷaṉa eṉpatum, avvāṟu teṟku nōkki vantu muttariyar eṉṟa ciṟappiṉik ki.pi.7-8ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē peṟṟu viṭṭaṉar eṉpatum, piṉṉar avarkaḷ celvākkiḻantu ciṟu talivarkaḷākap pēraracukaḷiṉ kīḻ aṭaṅki viṭṭaṉar eṉpatum, avvāṟu ciṟu talivarkaḷāka āyiṉapōtum celvākkuṭaṉ iruntaṉar eṉpataṟku avarkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭa cācaṉaṅkaḷum, avarkaḷip paṟṟiya cācaṉap pāṭalkaḷum cāṉṟu pakarkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum ituvari kūṟivanta ceytikaḷāl oru taliyāt tuṇiyappaṭum muṭivukaḷākum.


IV. tamiḻ nāṭṭil kaḷappirar
toṇṭi nāṭṭil kaḷappirar
ik kaḷappirar tamiḻ nāṭṭukkuḷ evvāṟu nuḻintaṉar? eṉṟa viṉāvi nōkkuvōm. ivarkaḷ āti pūrvam teṉ kaṉṉaṭap pakuti eṉap pārttōm. avvāṟāyiṉ aṉṟiya cūḻniliyil tamiḻ nāṭṭukkuḷ vantirukka vēṇṭum. kāraṇam aṉṟiya pallavanāṭu āntirap pakutiyi viṭṭut teṟkil pālāṟu vari paraviyiruntatu. pappatēvaṉ eṉṟa mutaṟ pallava maṉṉaṉ ivarkaḷi etirttu viraṭṭiyirukkiṟāṉ. piṉṉar civaskanta varmaṉ teṉ peṇṇi vari taṉ nāṭṭi virivākkap pakivari mēlum viraṭṭiyiruttal vēṇṭum. aṉṟiyum, āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil cimma viṣṇu pallavaṉik ‘kaḷappirari veṟṟikaṇṭa māvīraṉ’ eṉṟu iraṇṭām nantivarmaṉiṉ kācākkuṭic cācaṉam (culōkam 20) pukaḻkiṉṟatu.
iraṇṭām nanti varmap pallavaṉ kaḷappiraruṭaṉ pōriṭṭu veṉṟāṉ eṉṟa ceytikaḷi avaṉatu pullalūrc ceppēṭukaḷum (culōkam 6) paṭṭattāḷ maṅkalam ceppēṭukaḷum (culōkam 9) kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
iviyellām avarkaḷ pallava nāṭṭil nuḻintu nili peṟa muṭiyāmal teṟkil vantuviṭṭaṉar eṉpatiyē kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
cōṇāṭṭil kaḷappirar
avvāṟu teṉ kaṉṉaṭap pakutiyiliruntu viraṭṭappaṭṭup pallava nāṭṭil nuḻintu, aṅkum nilipeṟa iyalātu mēlum teṟku nōkki ōṭivanta kaḷappirar cōṇāṭṭukkuḷ nuḻintu viṭṭaṉar. tamiḻnāṭu vaṟumiyil toṭarntu irunta kālappakutiyē akkālam eṉak koḷḷalām. ikkālap pakutiyil karikālaṉukkup piṉṉar nikaḻnta cōṇāṭṭu araciyal nili eṉṉa eṉpati aṟitaṟku ēṟṟa ātāraṅkaḷ illi. celvākku iḻanta niliyiṉittāṉ nām uṇaramuṭiyum. tāyātic caṇṭi yellām naṭantu koṇṭiruntaṉa eṉṟum cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ avaṟṟil taliyiṭṭu muṟiceytāṉ eṉṟum ilakkiyac ceytikaḷ kūṟu
kiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, kuḻappamum iruntirukka vēṇṭum. atu kaḷappirar cōṇāṭṭil nilipeṟa eḷitāka ōr cūḻniliyit tantirukka vēṇṭum. mēlum, pukār nakaril taṅkaḷ talimiyi ēṟpaṭuttum aḷavukku avarkaḷ uyarntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatum orutali. ēṉeṉil, ki.pi.4ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ pakutikaḷil irunta puttatattar eṉṟa aracaṉatu aviyil taṉatu apimmāvatāram eṉṟa nūli araṅkēṟṟiyatāka annūlil kūṟic celkiṉṟār. eṉavē, kaḷappiratu āṭci nili peṟṟuviṭṭatu eṉpatu teḷivākiṉṟatu.
mēṟkaṇṭa kāraṇaṅkaḷiliruntu kaḷappirar kaṉṉaṭap pakutiyi
liruntu toṇṭi nāṭu vaḻiyākavē cōṇāṭṭup pukārvari vantiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatiyum, atukālic cōṇāṭṭil ciṟanta āṭci ēṟpaṭātatāl ivarkaḷ eḷitil annāṭṭil āṭciyik kippaṟṟi yiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatiyum, accutaṉ āṭcikku muṉpē kaḷappirar āṭci teṟkil kālkoṇṭu viṭṭatu eṉpatiyum avarkaḷ ki.pi.2-3 ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil taṅkaḷ araca yāttiriyiṉit tuvaṅki 4-5ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil cōṇāṭṭil niliyāka āṭci purintuḷḷaṉar eṉpatiyum teḷivāka uṇaralām. tamiḻnāṭṭil talinakari amittu āṭci ceyta kaḷappirar aracar
kaḷil peyar terintavaṉ pukārit talinakarākki āṇṭa accutak kaḷappāḷaṉ eṉṟa oruvaṉē eṉpati nām karututal vēṇṭum.
accuta kaḷappāḷaṉ
ivaṉip pukaḻvaṉavākap pala pāṭalkaḷ tamiḻ nūṟkaḷil kāṇak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṉatu tāyakam nantimāmalip pakutiyākum. ivaṉ taṉ āṭciyil makkaḷi ātarittuc ciṟanta pukaḻip peṟṟirukkiṉṟāṉ. ataṉāltāṉ,
 ‘	perumpukaḻ accutak kōvē
nantimā mali cilampa
nanti niṟparavutal nāvalark karitē.’
eṉṟu pāṭiyuḷḷaṉar pulavarkaḷ. ivaṉip pōṟṟip pukaḻutal mika aritu. ēṉeṉil, avaṉatu pukaḻi aḷaviṭa muṭiyātu. avvāṟu pukaḻuṭaṉ nanti malip pakutiyiṉi āṇṭa avaṉ ciṟitu ciṟitākat toṇṭi maṇṭalap pakutiyik kaṭantu pukāriṉik kippaṟṟip pēraracaṉāka vāḻntirukkiṉṟāṉ. tamiḻ mūvēntarum tam ciṟappukkaḷi iḻantu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. accutaṉ mūvēntariyum veṉṟu oru moḻi - ōrāṇi cellum āṭciyiṉi nili nāṭṭukiṉṟāṉ. ik karuttiṉi, 
	‘ aṭutiṟa loruvaniṟ paravutu meṅkōṉ
toṭu kaḻaṟkompūṭṭu pakaṭṭeḻiṉ mārpiṟ
kayaloṭu kalanta ciliyuṭik koṭuvarip
puyaluṟaḻ taṭakkip pōrvē laccutaṉ
toṉṟu mutirkaṭa lulakam
oṉṟupuri tikiri yuruṭṭuvō ṉeṉavē’
(kāriki pak.:112-113)
eṉṟu varum pāṭaṟ pakutiyāl aṟiyalām. tamiḻ nāvalar caritiyil accuta kaḷappāḷaṉ mūvēntariyum veṉṟāṉ eṉṟa kuṟippum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
accutaṉip paṟṟik kāṇappaṭum piṟitoru pāṭali nōkkuvōm atu,
	‘ tuvikkun tuḷimuṉṉīrk koṟki makaḷir
avippatam pallukku aḻakovvā muttam
maṇaṅkamaḻtār accutaṉ maṇkākkum vēliṉ
aṇaṅkamuta mannalār pāṭṭu’’ (kāriki pak.178)
eṉṟu amintuḷḷatu.
ivvāṟu oru kaḷappira aracaṉ pulavar perumakkaḷāl pōṟṟip pukaḻappaṭukiṉṟāṉ eṉiṉ, avaṉatu āṭciyiṉ ciṟappu enta aḷavukku uyarntirukka vēṇṭum? ati viṭuttu avarkaḷatu āṭciyil nalam aḻintu, tīmikaḷ malintu makkaḷ avatippaṭṭaṉar eṉṟu kūṟuvatu evvāṟṟāṉum ēṟpuṭittaṉṟu. araciyal amippiṉip potu niliyil niṉṟu nōkkuvōmāyiṉ enta oru aracum taṉatu celvākkiṉip parappattāṉ muyaṉṟirukkiṟatē aṉṟi veṉṟa nāṭṭiṉ araciyal, camutāya nilikaḷippōṟṟi ēṟṟukkoṇṭatāka illi. tamatu koḷkikaḷip pukutti nili nāṭṭiya piṉṉar tāṉ veṉṟa nāṭṭiṉ camutāya amippiṉi nōkkukiṉṟaṉar. ēṟpuṭiya tāyiṉ ataṉiyum ēṟṟuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar.
anta niliyiltāṉ, conta nāṭṭiliruntu katamparkaḷālum, toṇṭi nāṭṭiliruntu pallavarkaḷālum, viraṭṭi ōṭṭappaṭṭa kaḷappirarkaḷ, valikuṉṟiyirukkiṉṟa cōṇāṭṭaracarkaḷi eḷitil veṉṟu pukārit talinakarākki āṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. vaṟumiyiṉ koṭumiyil mayaṅkik kiṭanta pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉiyum kippaṟṟu kiṉṟaṉar. iviyaṉittum ki.pi. 2-3ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil kaḷappirar kālkoḷkiṉṟa kālattiya nikaḻccikaḷ. avarkaḷil ciṟappāka āṇṭu pukaḻpeṟṟa aracaṉ - peyar terinta paḻiya aracaṉ accuta kaḷappāḷaṉ eṉpavaṉē. tamiḻ ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷil kāṇum pala utāraṇac ceyyuṭkaḷ kaḷappāḷaṉip paṟṟic ciṟappittuk kūṟu kiṉṟaṉa.
pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil kaḷappirar
pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil kaḷappirar āṭci iruntatu eṉpataṟkum, avvāṭci iraṇṭu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukkum mēlāka niṉṟu nilaviyatu eṉpataṟkum, camutāyattil cīrkēṭukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭu nāṭu kuḻappa niliyil viṭappaṭṭatu eṉpataṟkum, camaya neṟikaḷ ellām nili taṭumāṟi viṭṭaṉa eṉpataṟkum, vēḷvikkuṭi, maturakara nallūrc cācaṉaṅkaḷil kuṟippukkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, kuṟippiṭṭa oru aracaṉ allatu enta enta aracarkaḷ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭik kippaṟṟi āṇṭārkaḷ eṉṟa ceyti teḷivākat terintilatu. āyiṉum, ‘karunāṭar’ eṉṟa toṭar kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭavarkaḷik kuṟikkum eṉiṉ periya purāṇattil cēkkiḻār kuṟikkum ‘karunāṭar’ eṉpatum kaṉṉaṭa tēcattavariyē cuṭṭum eṉpatil aiyamilli.
antak karunaṭa vēntaṉ nāṭṭik kippaṟṟum eṇṇattuṭaṉ tamiḻ nāṭuṭi maṉṉaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉi veṉṟu maturiyik kippaṟṟiṉāṉ eṉṟu cēkkiḻār kuṟittuc celkiṉṟār
	“ kāṉak kaṭicūḻ vaṭukakkaru nāṭar kāval
māṉap paṭimaṉṉaṉ valintu nilaṅkoḷ vāṉāy
yāṉik kutirik karuvippaṭi vīrar tiṇtēr
cēṉik kaṭaluṅ koṭuteṉ ticinōkki vantāṉ.
(pe.mū.nā.pu.11)
eṉṟum,
	‘ vantuṟṟa perumpaṭi maṇputiyap parappic
cantappoti yiltamiḻ nāṭuṭi maṉṉaṉ vīram
cintacceru veṉṟu taṉṉāṇi celuttu māṟṟāl
kantappoḻilcūḻ maturāpuri kāval koṇṭāṉ’
(pe.mū.nā.pu.12)
eṉṟum varukiṉṟa pāṭalkaḷ icceytikaḷik kuṟippaṉavē.
ivvāṟu pāṇṭiya nāṭṭik kippaṟṟiya ivarkaḷ aruka camayattiṉic cārntu niṉṟavarkaḷāyc civa neṟikaḷiyum paṇikaḷiyum taṭi ceykiṉṟaṉar. antaṇarkaḷukkuk koṭukkap
paṭṭa piramatēyaṅkaḷip paṟimutal ceytaṉar eṉṟu muṉpu pārttōm. civa vaḻipāṭukaḷi nikaḻātavāṟu taṭutta ceytikaḷi,
	‘ paṭināṉ kuṭaṉṟu pañcavaṉ turantu
maturi vavviya karunaṭar vēntaṉ
arukarc cārntu niṉṟu araṉpaṇi aṭippa’
eṉṟu varum ‘kallāṭac ceyyuṭ pakuti teḷivākak kūṟukiṉṟatu.
eṉavē, pāṇṭiya nāṭṭi ivarkaḷ veṉṟaṉar; civa camaya neṟikaḷit taṭuttaṉar; taṅkaḷ aruka camayattit taḻuvip pōṟṟiṉar eṉṟa ceytikaḷiyum, maturi āṭciyiṉiyum kippaṟṟik kāval koṇṭaṉar eṉpatiyum nām uṇaralām.


V. kaḷappirar kalikaḷ
kaḷappirar camayam
ivarkaḷ camayam camaṇam eṉpatik kaṇṭōm. cōṇāṭṭilum, pāṇṭiya nāṭṭilum taṅkaḷatu araciyal celvākkiṉip parappiya ivarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ taḻuviyirunta camaṇa camayattip pōṟṟiyum, moḻiyiṉip pukuttiyum vaḷarkkap perumuyaṟci ceytaṉar. camaṇa caṅkaṅkaḷ āṅkāṅku eḻuntaṉa. camaṇa muṉivarkaḷ makkaḷāl matikkap peṟṟaṉar. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ cilappatikārattil camaṇa nōṉpikaḷip paṟṟi āṅkāṅkuk kūṟic celvati nōkkac camaṇa camayam tamiḻnāṭṭil celvākkuṭaṉ tikaḻntatu eṉpati eḷitil uṇaralām. ivarkaḷ kālattil cilampi oṭṭiya kālattil, eḻunta maṇimēkali putta camayac celvākkiṉi uṇartti niṟkiṉṟatu. eṉavē, camaṇamum, puttamum ōṅkiya niliyil iruntaṉa eṉpati aṟiyalām.
ki.mu.2ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal ki.pi. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari kiṭikkum pirāmit tamiḻk kalveṭṭukaḷil kāṇappeṟum ceyti kaḷik koṇṭu, kaṭic caṅka kālattilēyē tamiḻ maṉṉarkaḷiṉ celvākkil camaṇa, putta camayaṅkaḷ ōṅkiyiruntaṉa eṉpati aṟiyalām. cilavaṟṟip pārppōm.
karuvūr arukil āranāṭṭār maliyil kiṭitta oru kalveṭṭu cēra aracarkaḷ kālattuca camaṇac celvākkiṉik kāṭṭu kiṉṟatu.
	‘ amaṇṇaṉ yāṟṟūr ceṅkāyapaṉ uṟiya
kō ātaṉ cērallirum poṟi makaṉ
peruṅkaṭuṅkō makaṉiḷaṅ
kaṭuṅkō iḷaṅkō ākaaṟutta kal’ 
(kalveṭṭukkaḷ pak.7)
ituvē antac cācaṉam. ceṅkāyapaṉ eṉṟa amaṇṇaṉ patiṟṟuppattiṉ ēḻu, eṭṭu, oṉpatu ākiya pattukkaḷil pāṭap
peṟum vāḻiyātaṉ, peruñcēralirumpoṟi, iḷañcēral irum poṟi ākiya aracarkaḷiṉ kālattil vāḻntavaṉ. ic cācaṉam ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭic cārntatu. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kālattuc camaṇac celvākkiṉic cilappatikārattāl uṇaralām.
piṉvarum cācaṉam pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil camaṇac celvāk
kiruntamiyik kāṭṭum,
	‘ kaṇi nanta āciriya i
kuvaṉke tammam i
ttaa neṭiñ caḻiyaṉ pa
ṇaaṉ kaṭalaaṉ vaḻutti
y koṭṭu pitta paḷiiy.’    (tamiḻ pirāmik kalveṭṭukkaḷ)
itu kaṇi nanta ācirikaṉ eṉṟa camaṇaṉukkuk koṭukkap
paṭṭa tarmam. itic ceytu tantavaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ. ivaṉukku paṇaaṉ, kaṭalaaṉ, vaḻutti eṉṟa ciṟappup peyarkaḷik koṭuttuk kallil veṭṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
	‘ cemmaluḷḷan turattaliṟ kaṟuttōr
oḷiṟu vēlaḻuvaṅ kaḷiṟu paṭakkaṭakkum
māvaṇ kaṭalaṉ viḷaṅkil’ (akam:81)
eṉṟa akanāṉūṟṟup pāṭaṟ pakutiyil ālampēri cāttaṉār taliyāṉaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñ
ceḻiyaṉi ‘māvaṇ kaṭalaṉ’ eṉṟu viḷittiruppatiyum, ic cācaṉattil kaṭalaṉ eṉṟu varuvatiyum oppu nōkkuka. mēlum, itē pulavar ‘vāṉavaṉ maṟavaṉ piṭṭaṉi’p pāṭiyiruppatum, karuvūrk karukil piṭṭaṅ koṟṟaṉ cācaṉam oṉṟu kiṭittiruppatum tamiḻ nāṭṭup paṇṭi varalāṟṟil oru kālak kuṟippiṉi uṟutip paṭuttu vataṟku ēṟpa uḷḷaṉa. ic cācaṉattiṉ kālam ki.mu. 100 eṉak karutu kiṉṟaṉar.
	‘ vaciyil vempōr vāṉavaṉ maṟavaṉ
naciyiṉ vāḻnarkku naṉkalañ curakkum
poyyā vāyvāṭ puṉikaḻaṟ piṭṭaṉ’ (akam: 143)
eṉpatu ālampēri cāttaṉār vāṉavaṉ maṟavaṉ piṭṭaṉip paṟṟi akanāṉūṟṟil pāṭic cellum ceyti.
ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu māvaṭṭam tirunātar kuṉṟil kiṭikkum oru pirāmi - vaṭṭeḻuttut tamiḻca cācaṉam camaṇa āciriyar oruvar uṇṇāviratamiruntu uyir nītta ceytiyit terivik
kiṉṟatu. ic cācaṉattiṉ kālam pirāmi eḻuttu vaḷarcciyiṉ iṟuti niliyiyum vaṭṭeḻuttut tōṟṟattiṉ mutal niliyiyum kāṭṭum kālam eṉkiṉṟaṉar. atu ki.pi.4-5ām nūṟṟāṇṭākalām. cācaṉam piṉvarumāṟu amintuḷḷatu.
‘ aimpat tēḻ aṉa
caṉan nōṟṟa
cantira nantiā
cirikar nicītiki’ 				
camaṇa muṉivarkaḷ uṇṇā nōṉpu iruntu uyir tuṟappar eṉṟa ceytiyiṉiyum, ataṉ niṉivāka makkaḷ avarkaḷukku niṉivuc ciṉṉam eḻuppukiṉṟaṉar eṉpatiyum ic cācaṉattāl aṟiyalām.
	‘uṇṇā nōṉpō ṭuyar patippeyarttatum’
(cilappatikāram; nīrppaṭik kāti:83)
eṉṟa cilappatikārac ceytiyiṉiyum, caṅkappāṭalkaḷil kāṇum vaṭakkiruttaliyum, itaṉuṭaṉ oppiṭṭāl ippaḻakkam aṉi varālum kaṭippiṭikkappaṭṭu vantatu eṉpatit teriyalām.
eṉavē, ki.mu.mutal nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavilēyē camaṇa camayam tamiḻ nāṭṭil kāl koṇṭu viṭṭatu eṉpatiyum, araciyal iṭiyīṭu ēṟpaṭumuṉṉē camayam pukuntuviṭṭatu eṉpatiyum teḷivāka uṇaralām.
ivvāṟu ivarkaḷ aruka camayattic cārntiruntatōṭu, civa neṟikaḷi vaḷarāmal taṭi ceytaṉar eṉpatiyum aṟikiṟōm.
	‘ maturi vavviya karunaṭa vēntar
arukarc cārntu araṉpaṇi aṭippa’
eṉṟu varukiṉṟa kallāṭac ceyyuṭ pakuti icceytiyiṉi uṟutip paṭuttum.
ikkālap pakutiyilēyē camaṇa camayattuṭaṉ puttamum tamiḻ nāṭṭil paraviyiruntatu. ‘ālam pēri cāttaṉār’,āvūrk kāvitikaḷ cātēvaṉār, ‘karuvūrk katappiḷḷic cāttaṉār’, ‘peruntalic cāttaṉār’ ‘cīttalic cāttaṉār’ eṉṟellām payilappaṭukiṉṟa pulavar peyarkaḷ putta camayac cārpiṉik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. maṇimēkali puttac celvākkil tōṉṟiya kāviyam aṉṟō? iccamayamum ivarkaḷ āṭciyil ciṟappākavē iruntuḷḷatu. puttatattar vāḻntatum, avaratu ‘apimmāvatāram’ tōṉṟi araṅkēṟiyatum ikkālattiltāṉ. innūlil avar kaḷappira aracaṉ accuta vikrantaṉ pukār nakarit talinakarākak koṇṭu cōṇāṭṭi āṇṭāṉ eṉṟa kuṟippiṉi nūlil tantuḷḷār eṉpatiyellām muṉṉar kaṇṭōm.
kaḷappirar araciyal
ivvāṟākac camayamum, camayak koḷkikaḷum nāṭṭil paravi vantatupōla, muṟiyāṉatoru āṭci amittu āṇṭaṉar eṉṟu colvataṟkēṟṟa ātāraṅkaḷ illi eṉalām. kaḷappirar āṭci paṟṟi aṟiyat tuṇiyākum orē nūl puttatattaratu apimmāva tāramē. mēlē itip paṟṟik kūṟiṉōm.
tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum ilakkaṇa mēṟkōḷ pāṭalkaḷilum āṅkāṅku accutaṉip paṟṟiyum avaṉatu ciṟappāṉa āṭci yiṉip paṟṟiyum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, ciṟanta araciyal amippil toṭarntu āṭci ceytu tampukaḻ parappiṉar eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu. pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil ivarkaḷ ōraḷavu celvākkup peṟṟa kālattil tāṉ camaṇa caṅkam kūṭiyirukka vēṇṭum. akkālam ki.pi.4-5 ām nūṟṟāṇṭākalām.
eṉiṉum, ikkālap pakutiyil kaḷappirar āṭci muṟi, araciyal nirvākaṅkaḷ evvāṟu iruntaṉa? yāryār āṭcipīṭam ēṟi āḷum niliyiṉip peṟṟaṉar? akkālac cūḻniliyil makkaḷiṉ nili evvāṟu iruntatu? eṉṟa ceytikaḷ yāvum iṉṉum viḷaṅkāta putirākavē uḷḷaṉa.
moḻi ilakkiyam
ikkālap pakutiyil celvākkup peṟṟa iccamayaṅkaḷ pākatam, pāli ākiya moḻikaḷāl āṉa ilakkiyaṅkaḷip peṟṟiruntaṉa. nāṭṭil im moḻikaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyum ātikkamum paraviṉa. tamiḻmoḻi amippu ammoḻiyamippukku ēṟpat tiriyat toṭaṅ kiṟṟu. camaṇa, putta muṉivarkaḷ pākata moḻiyil eṇṇit tamiḻ moḻiyip poṟittaṉar. itaṟkuc cāṉṟākak ki.pi.2ām nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷiyiṭap peṟṟatākiya cātavākaṉac catakarṇiyiṉ pākatat tamiḻ irumoḻi nāṇayam ivvamippiṉi allatu moḻi peyarppiṉik koṇṭirukkiṉṟatu.
	‘ vasiṭi putasa siri satakaṇisa rāñō
vaciṭṭi makaṉku tiru catakaṇiru aracaṉku.’
ikkācu vaciṭṭi putra srīcatakarṇi aracaṉuṭiyatu eṉpatip pātakam, tamiḻākiya moḻikaḷil poṟittuḷḷaṉar.
piṉṉar ik kuravarkaḷ vaṭṭāra moḻiyilēyē tamatu karuttuk kaḷip parappa eṇṇi nalla tamiḻp pulavarkaḷākat taṅkaḷi ākkik koṇṭaṉar. ikkaḷappirar āṭcik kālatti oṭṭi eḻuntaviyē patiṉeṇkīḻk kaṇakku nūṟkaḷ. ivaṟṟil cila camaṇa camayak koḷkikaḷik koṇṭum, cila camaṇa muṉivarkaḷāl iyaṟṟap peṟṟum uḷḷaṉa. nālvaki uṟutip poruḷkaḷil aṟamum, vīṭum ivvaki nūlkaḷil peritum pōṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. eṉiṉum, tamiḻ ilakkaṇa, ilakkiya marapukku ēṟpaṭa akattiṇip pāṭalkaḷiyum, yāppu, aṇi ilakkaṇa nūṟkaḷiyum vakuttaṉar. cīvaka cintāmaṇi, peruṅkati, cūḷāmaṇi mutalāṉavi 8, 9 ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil tōṉṟiya camaṇak kāviyaṅkaḷē. yāpparuṅkalam tanta pulavaruma oru camaṇa muṉivarē.

camayak kalvi
pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil kaḻuku maliyil kāṇappeṟum nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa vaṭṭeḻuttuc cācaṉaṅkaḷ camaṇa camayak kuravarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivi ki.pi. eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavil tōṉṟiyavi. ivaṟṟi nōkkumpōtu, ivarkaḷiṉ kīḻ camayak kalvi - potuk kalvi peṟap pala māṇākkarkaḷum, māṇakkiyar kaḷum iruntaṉar eṉpatiyum, camayak kuravarkaḷāka āṭavarēyaṉṟip peṇpālarum iruntirukkiṉṟaṉar eṉpatiyum teḷiyalām. ivi yellām camaṇa camayam makkaḷiṭiyē koṇṭuḷḷa perumatippik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. araciyal nili nīṭittu nilipeṟa muṭiyāmal pōy viṭṭālum kūṭa camayanili akkālac cūḻniliyil vērūṉṟi yiruntatu eṉpatu orutali.
vēṟṟup pulattavarāṉa kaḷappirar tamiḻnāṭṭup paṇpāṭṭu muṟikaḷi ēṟṟuk koḷḷātatil viyappoṉṟumilli. tamiḻ maṉṉarkaḷ ceyta tāṉaṅkaḷiyellām naṭavāmal taṭuttuppaṟittut tam camayattukkuk koṭuttaṉar eṉṟa ceytikaḷik kaṇṭōm. civa camayam ivarkaḷ kālattil ātarippār aṟṟut tāḻnta niliyil iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpati arukarc cārntu araṉ paṇi aṭitta nikaḻccikaḷāl uṇaralām.
piṟkālattil pallavarkaḷ camaṇa camayatti orō kālaṅkaḷil taḻuviyiruntālum, vaḷarntu vantu koṇṭirunta pakti iyakkattiṉ vēkattiṉāl camaṇa camayamum taṉ celvākkiṉi iḻakkalāyiṟṟu. pallava vēntaṉ makēntiravarmaṉ varalāṟṟiṉiyum, pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉ kūṉpāṇṭiyaṉ varalāṟṟiṉiyum ataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷākak kāṭṭalām.


VI. kaḷappirar - camaṇam cīraḻivu
piṟkālattil ivarkaḷil cilar camaṇam viṭṭuc civa camayattiṉit taḻuviṉar eṉpati aṟikiṟōm. tirunāvukkaracar tarumacēṉar eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ camaṇattilē ciṟappāṉa niliyil iruntatiyum piṉṉar iṟivaṉ tiruvaruḷāl civa camayattukku māṟiṉār eṉpatiyum, atukālik kāñciyil āṭci naṭatti vanta makēntira varma pallavaṉum camaṇa camayam viṭṭuc civaṉ āyiṉāṉ eṉpatiyum periya purāṇac ceytikaḷāl aṟiyalām.
itupōḻtu pāṇṭi nāṭu eppaṭiyiruntatu eṉpati,
	‘ pūḻiyar tamiḻnāṭ ṭuḷḷa poruvil cīrp patikaḷ ellām
pāḻiyum arukar mēvum paḷḷikaḷ palavumākic
cūḻiruṭ kuḻukkaḷ pōlat toṭimayiṟ pīliyōṭu
mūḻinīr kiyiṟ paṟṟi amaṇarē yāki moyppa ’
(periya purāṇam ti.ca.pu.: 601)
eṉṟa pāṭalāl aṟiyalām. pāṇṭiya māṟavarmaṉ muṉpu camaṇaṉāka iruntu ñāṉacampantarālum, mantiri kulacciṟiyār, maṉivi maṅkiyarkkaraciyārālum civaṉākiṉāṉ. ivaṉatu niliyi, 
	‘ variccilit teṉṉa vaṉtāṉ uytaṟku vaṭavar kōmāṉ
tiruvuyirt taruḷum celvap pāṇṭimā tēvi yārum
kurikaḻal amicca ṉārām kulacciṟi yārum eṉṉum
iruvartam pāṅku maṉṟic civamaṅ keytā tāka’
(periyapurāṇam ti.ca.pu. 603)
eṉṟu viḷakkukiṉṟār cēkkiḻār.
iraṇṭām nantivarmap pallavaṉ antaṇarkaḷukku iṟiyili yāka nilap pakutikaḷit tarumpōtu ciṉak kaṭuṅ kōṭpāṭukaḷi allatu atarmak kōṭpāṭukaḷi nīkkik koṭuttāṉ eṉṟu utayēntiram cācaṉam kūṟukiṟatu.
‘inta ellikaṭkuḷ paṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷiyum, nīrppācaṉa urimiyōṭum, ciṉak kaṭuṅkōṭpāṭukaḷi vilakkiyummaṉṉaṉ koṭuttāṉ’
itu utayēntirac ceppēṭṭup pakuti.
kaṅka aracaṉ, srī rāmāṉujarāl camaṇa camayam viṭṭu viṇava neṟiyit taḻuviṉāṉ eṉṟu ‘koṅku tēca rājākkaḷiṉ caritam’ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
iviyellām camaṇa camayattiṉ celvākku ciṟitu ciṟitāka maṟintu varukiṉṟateṉpatiyum, ati ūkkavum, vaḷarttu ātaravu taravum kūṭiyavarkaḷ illātoḻintaṉar eṉpatiyum kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. camaṇarkaḷukkup pala vaḻikaḷilum etirppukaḷum iṭiyūṟukaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. pallavar kālattil tōṉṟiya camayap puraṭci intu camaya vaḷarccikku uṟutuṇiyāka niṉṟatu. camaṇarkaḷ kaḻuvēṟṟap paṭṭārkaḷ pāṇṭi nāṭṭil ikkaruttiṉi,
	‘ paṇpuṭi amicca ṉārum pāruḷōr aṟiyum āṟṟāl
kaṇpuṭi paṭṭu nīṇṭa kaḻuttaṟi niriyil ēṟṟa
naṇpuṭi ñāṉam uṇṭār maṭattuttī nāṭi iṭṭa
eṇperuṅ kuṉṟat teṇṇā yiravarum ēṟi ṉārkaḷ’
(periyapurāṇam ti.ca.pu. 855)
eṉṟa periya purāṇap pāṭal viḷakkum.
pōlic camaṇarkaḷāl aṉal vātam, puṉal vātam eṉṟa vātaṅkaḷukku īṭukoṭukka muṭiyavilli. tattuva vicāraṅkaḷ camaṇarkaḷiṭam kuṟintuviṭṭaṉa. avarkaḷatu tikamparattaṉmi makkaḷāl veṟukkappaṭṭatu. ñāṉacampantarum, apparum mikak kaṭumiyākavum iḻicoṟkaḷālum avarkaḷi ēci makkaḷiṭam matippiḻakkac ceytaṉar. ivvāṟāka araciyal niliyiliruntum, camutāyattiṉiṉṟum, avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ camayac celvākkiṉiyum iḻantu cīraḻiyalāyiṉar.

VII. piṟkālattil kaḷappāḷar
nāṭōṭikaḷāka vanta ikkaḷappirar taṅkaḷatu āṭciyum, araciyalum, camayamum ellām kāla veḷḷattil mūḻkat tāḻcci yuṟṟut tamiḻar tam vāḻkki muṟikaḷuṭaṉ iṇintuviṭṭaṉar eṉṟē collalām. avvāṟu tamiḻar vāḻvuṭaṉ iṇintu tamiḻ nāṭṭup pakutikaḷil nilittu vāḻat talippaṭṭa kālattiltāṉ tamiḻ makkaḷ kalācārattuṭaṉ oṉṟi vēṟupāṭuṇara muṭiyāta aḷavukkuk kalantu vāḻkiṉṟa cūḻniliyi aṭikiṉṟaṉar.
piṟkāla aracarkaḷiṭam celvākkup peṟṟu araciyal aluval kaḷi ēṟṟum, paṇintum vāḻntaṉar. aracarkaḷ ‘kaḷappāḷarācar’ eṉṟa paṭṭam koṭuttuc ciṟappukkaḷ ceykiṉṟa aḷavukkut taṅkaḷ celvākkiṉi uyarttik koṇṭaṉar. cilar tamiḻp pulavar kaḷākavum, cilar tamiḻp pulavarkaḷi ātarikkum vaḷḷalkaḷākavum iruntirukkiṉṟaṉar. perumaṉṉarkaḷiṉ paṭikaḷukkut talimi tāṅkit tuṇipurintu vantirukkiṉṟaṉar.
vēḷāṇ marapu avarkaḷiṉ camutāya vāḻvil taṉiyiṭattip peṟṟateṉṟē collalām. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vēḷāṇ marapil kaḷappirarkaḷ kalanta piṉṉar kaḷappirarik kaḷappāḷar eṉṟa peyaril aḻikkalāyiṉar. tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kaḷappāḷartāṉ kaḷappirar eṉṟu cilar mayaṅkit tali taṭumāṟik kūṟiyuḷḷatiyum nōkkuka. piṟkālac cācaṉaṅkaḷ ‘kaḷappāḻar’ eṉṟē avarkaḷik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.
ivvāṟu avarkaḷ tamatu paḻiya celvākkukaḷiyellām, iḻantu - maṟantu - putiya amippil taṅkaḷi īṭupaṭuttik koṇṭu vāḻat talippaṭṭaṉar. avvāṟu kaḷappāḷarāka vāḻnta oru cila talivarkaḷip purāṇaṅkaḷum, ilakkiya nūṟkaḷum kuṟippiṭu kiṉṟaṉa.
kūṟṟuva nāyaṉār
ivar periya purāṇattil cēkkiḻārāl pāṭappeṟṟa aracar. ivar tam āṭcik kālattil ulakelām tamatu oru kuṭikkīḻk koṇarntu kāval ceytār eṉṟu avar kūṟukiṉṟār.
	‘ kātaṟ perumit toṇṭiṉ nilikkaṭalcūḻ viyam
             kāttaḷittuk
kōtaṅ kakala muyalkaḷantik kūṟṟa ṉārtam kaḻal            vaṇaṅki..’ 
(periya purāṇam kūṟ:nā:8)
eṉṟu avar taṉatu nūlil kuṟittuḷḷār.
	‘ ōtam taḻuviya ñālamellām oru kōvil vittāṉ
kōti neṭuvēl kaḷappāḷaṉ ākiya kūṟṟuvaṉē’
eṉavē, kūṟṟuva kaḷappāḷaṉ ulakit taṉṉaṭik kīḻk koṇṭu āṇṭa ceyti teḷivāka uḷḷatu.
mūrtti nāyaṉār
ivarum cēkkiḻārāl periya purāṇattil pāṭap peṟṟavarē. ivarik karunaṭa vēntaṉ eṉṟu avar kuṟikkiṉṟār.
	‘ kāṉak kaṭicūḻ vaṭukakkaru nāṭar kāval
māṉap paṭimaṉṉaṉ valintu nilaṅkoḷ vāṉāy
yāṉik kutirik karuvippaṭi vīrar tiṇtēr
cēṉik kaṭalum koṭuteṉ ticinōkki vantāṉ’
(periya purāṇam mūrtti nāyaṉār : 11)
eṉpatu pāṭal. ivar nālvakip paṭiyum parappi vaṭakkiṉiṉṟum pōntu maturiyik kippaṟṟi āṇṭatākak kūṟukiṉṟār. ivaratu maturippaṭiyeṭuppiṉip paṟṟip ‘pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil kaḷappirar’ eṉṟa pakutiyil muṉpum kuṟippiṭṭōm.
cēkkiḻāṉ pālaṟāvāyaṉ kaḷappāḷarāyar
ivar periyapurāṇa āciriyar cēkkiḻār perumāṉiṉ tampiyāvār. iraṇṭām, mūṉṟām kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉiṉ iraṇṭāvatu āṭci āṇṭil kōṭṭūrc civaṉ kōyilil nantā viḷakku eriya vikkap poṟkācukaḷ nivantamāka aḷittuḷḷār.
kaḷappāḷarācaṉ neṟkuṉṟa vāṇaṉ
ivaratu peyarik koṇṭē ivar neṟkuṉṟam eṉṟa ūrukkut talivarāka iruntār eṉpati aṟiyalām. ivar kulōttuṅkaṉ kālattavar. ciṟanta vaḷḷalākavum, pulavarākavum, nalla oḻukka cīlarākavum viḷaṅkiyavar. cōḻaṉatu āṭcik kālattil eḻunta tiruppukalūrk kalveṭṭu icceytikaḷit terivikkiṉṟatu.
ivar ikkōyilil kuṭi koṇṭirukkum iṟivaṉ mītu oru antāti pāṭiyuḷḷār. avvantātiyiṉ iṟutiyil annūlip pāṭiṉavaṉ neṟkuṉṟa vāṇaṉ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu.
‘kaḷappāḷaṉ neṟkuṉṟa vāṇaṉ antātik kalittuṟiyē.
eṉṟa variyiṉik koṇṭu acceytiyit teḷiyalām.
ivaratu arumperum paṇpukaḷi viḷakka vanta pulavar purāṇa āciriyar kumaṇaṉ, teḷḷā ṟeṟinta nantivarmaṉ ākiya maṉṉarkaḷuṭaṉ oppiṭṭup pāṭukiṉṟār.
‘ talikoṭutta kumaṇaṉilum uyirkoṭutta nantiyilum
tamiḻkāttāṉ pōy
vilipaṭumin neṟkuṉṟa vāṇaṉimēl eṉattuṇintu
								viḷampa lāki
nilipoṟutta pulamiyuṟṟu māṉattum pukaḻ peritā
niṉittālum
kaliyuṇarcci ciṟituṟiṉum iṉatto ṭikalkoṭuṅ
kuṇamum kaṭantāṉammā’
eṉṟu avaṉatu tamiḻp paṟṟiṉ perumiyiṉic ciṟappittup pāṭic ceṉṟuḷḷār.
kaḷappāḷarācaṉ cāli
tiruppukalūrk kalveṭṭu, kaḷappāḷa rājar eṉpār cāli ceyvittār eṉṟum, aṅkup pirāmaṇarkaḷ uṇpataṟkākac cālippuṟamāka iṟiyili nilam aḷittār eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟatu. itic ceyta kaḷappāḷarācaṉ neṟkuṉṟa vāṇaṉ. ātiyil kaḷappirar tamiḻaracarkaḷāl koṭukkappaṭṭa pirama tēyaṅkaḷik kippaṟṟik koṇṭaṉar eṉṟu vēḷvikkuṭi cācaṉam kūṟukiṟatu.
 ‘ aḷavariya ātirācari akalanīkki akaliṭattik
kaḷapraṉ eṉṉum kaliyaricaṉ kik koṇṭataṉi
							iṟakkiyapiṉ’
eṉṟu vēḷvikkuṭic cācaṉap pakuti īṇṭu oppu nōkkaṟ pālatē.
accuta kaḷappāḷar
ivar mēṟkūṟiya accuta kaḷappāḷaṉilum vēṟupaṭṭavar. ivaratu kālam ki.pi.12ām nūṟṟāṇṭu civañāṉapōtam eḻutiya mey kaṇṭāriṉ tantiyāvar. eṉavē, meykaṇṭāriyum ikkulatta varākavē koḷḷa iṭamēṟpaṭukiṟatu. camaṇa camaya tattuvaṅ koṇṭa paḻimiyiṉiyum civa cittānta neṟikaṇṭa ikkālac cūḻniliyiṉiyum oppu nōkki uṇarka.
muṭivu
itu kāṟum, kaḷappirarkaḷ evvāṟu moḻi peyar tēyattu vēṅkaṭattumpar karunaṭanāṭṭiṉ araciyal celvākkiṉip peṟṟuc ciṟappuṟṟum,piṉ palvēṟu araciyal cūḻalil taṅkaḷi iḻantu tamiḻ makkaḷuṭaṉ kalantu tamiḻ nāṭṭil caṅkamam āyiṉar eṉpatiyum, itēpōlac camaṇa camayamum evvāṟu nilipeṟa muṭiyāmal pōyiṟṟu eṉpatiyum oruvāṟu ārāyntu kūṟiṉōm.


vēḷvikkuṭic ceppēṭukaḷ
‘vēḷvikkuṭic ceppēṭukaḷ’ intiyak kalveṭṭu patiṉēḻām tokutiyil patiṉāṟāvatu eṇṇākap patippikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa1.iti āṅkila eḻuttākka muṟiyil patippittavar ec.kiruṣṇa cāstiri, pi.ē., āvār. iti es. irājam nūl veḷiyīṭṭuk kuḻuviṉar ‘cācaṉamāli’ eṉṟa ciṟu nūlil veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.2 kālañceṉṟa tiru.kū.ē. catāciva paṇṭārattār tamatu ‘pāṇṭiyar varalāṟu’ eṉṉum nūlil cērkki 1l patippittuḷḷār.3 ivikaḷ aṉittum, ceppēṭṭiṉi veḷiyiṭṭu ulakukku aṟivikka vēṇṭum eṉṟa nōkkamum, varalāṟṟukkut tuṇiyāka amintiruppatik kāṭṭum nōkkamum uṭiyaṉa. 
eṉavē, avarkaḷ cācaṉattiṉ ceyyuḷ naṭiyiṉi avvaḷavu karutavilli. tamiḻc ceyyuḷ eṭṭāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil evvāṟu amintiruntatu. atu cācaṉaṅkaḷil peṟṟirunta iṭam ettakiyatu eṉpatiyellām ārāyvataṟku iccācaṉam oru ciṟanta karuviyāka miḷirukiṟatu.
iccācaṉam kāṭṭukiṉṟa araca vaḻiyiṉar, pōr nikaḻccikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum, aracu muṟiyiṉiyum kūrntu nōkkiṉāl eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyuḷḷa tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟiṉi mikat teḷivāka uṇaralām. iccācaṉattiṉ varalāṟṟuc ciṟappiṉiyum naṉku uṇaralām.
ivvāṟāṉa pala teḷivukaḷukkākac cācaṉattip pala pakutikaḷākat talippiṭṭuḷḷēṉ.
mutal pakuti, caṅkakālap pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟa palyākacāli mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti1 eṉpāṉ vēḷvi cey vataṟkākak koṟkik kiḻāṉāṉa naṟkoṟṟaṉ eṉpāṉukkup ‘pākaṉūr’2 eṉṟa ceḻippu mikka ūri ‘vēḷvikkuṭi’ eṉṟu peyariṭṭut tāṉam ceytatiyum, piṉṉar ‘kaḷappirar’3 eṉṟa ōr iṉattavar nāṭṭiṉik kippaṟṟik koḷḷa, antat tāṉam paṟikkappaṭṭatiyum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
aṭutta pakuti, ki.pi. 575 mutal 700 vari āṇṭa pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷiṉ parampariyiyum, avarkaḷ cēra, cōḻa, pallava, cāḷukkiya maṉṉarōṭum, ciṟu talivarkaḷōṭum ceyta pōrkaḷip paṟṟiyum, avarkaḷ ceyta tāṉac ceytikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟik kuṟittum kūṟukiṟatu. intap pakutiyē varalāṟṟukku iṉṟiyamiyāta pakutiyākum.
piṉṉar varukiṉṟa pakuti, neṭuñcaṭiyaṉatu mūṉṟāvatu āṭciyāṇṭil ‘kāmakkāṇi naṟciṅkaṉ’ eṉpavaṉ paḻimiyatāṉa antak koṭikku urimi kōri muṟiyiṭuvatiyum ataṟku aracaṉ cāṉṟu kāṭṭumpaṭi kūṟa, avaṉum aracaṉatu āṇiyiṉ
paṭi cāṉṟu kāṭṭiṭa maṉṉaṉ makiḻntu, tam muṉṉōr koṭutta paṭiyē ellā vakiyāṉa urimikaḷōṭum tāri vārttuk koṭuttatiyum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
ataṉpiṉ, tāṉam ceyta nilattiṉ ellikaḷi variyaṟuk kiṉṟa ceytiyiyum, intat tāṉattic ceyalpaṭuttit tantavaṉ neṭuñcaṭiyaṉatu amiccaṉ ‘māṟaṅkāri’4 eṉpatiyum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
iṟutiyil tāṉattip pakirntukoḷḷa vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ iṉṉiṉṉār eṉpatiyum, aḷaviṉiyum iccācaṉatti eḻutiyavaṉ peyariyum kāṇalām.


kuṟippukkaḷum viḷakkaṅkaḷum
1. EPigraphia India Volume 17, No.16
2. cācaṉamāli: pak:7, es.irājam, 5, tampuc ceṭṭit
teru, ceṉṉi - 1 (1960)
3. pāṇṭiyar varalāṟu, pak:193, 5ām patippu kū.ē. catāciva paṇṭārattār (1966)
4. palyāka mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti
ivaṉip pāṭuvaṉavākap puṟanāṉūṟṟil 5 pāṭalkaḷ iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivaṉ yākam pala ceytavaṉ eṉpatip puṟam 15āvatu pāṭalil neṭṭimiyār eṉṟa pulavar kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār. maturik kāñciyilum ivaṉip paṟṟiya kuṟippu uḷḷatu. ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷ koṇṭu aṟiyalākum. pāṇṭiya aracarkaḷuḷ toṉmi
cāṉṟavaṉ ivaṉē.
5. pākaṉūr
toṉmip perumi vāyntatu. ki.pi.2ām nūṟṟāṇṭup pirāmit tamiḻk kalveṭṭukkaḷil ippeyar kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. iṉṟiya pākaṉēriyē paṇṭu pākaṉūr eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu.
6. kaḷappirar
kaṭic caṅka kālattukkup piṉ ki.pi.3ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal ki.pi.6ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari tamiḻ nāṭṭil āṭci ceytavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷatu āṭcikkālattil tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ nili, tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ nili, tamiḻmaṉṉarkaḷiṉ nili yāvum viḷakkamaṟṟuk kiṭantaṉa. ivarkaḷip paṟṟi oru teḷivāṉa ceytiyum kiṭṭiṟṟilli. ataṉāl akkālap pakutiyit tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ iruṇṭakālam eṉpar.
7. māṟaṅkāri
ivaṉ kaḷakkuṭi (iṉṟiya kaḷakkāṭu) nāṭṭic cārntavaṉ. vittiya kulattil piṟantavaṉ. pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñcaṭiyaṉatu amiccaṉum, cēṉātipatiyumāvāṉ. ivaṉ maturi naraciṅkapurattil oru kaṟkōyil kaṭṭat toṭaṅki atu muṭiyāta pōtē iṟantu paṭṭāṉ. piṉ avaṉ tampi māṟaṉ eyiṉaṉ appaṇiyic ceytu muṭittāṉ eṉṟu āṉimalik kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.


cācaṉap pakuti
tāṉattiṉ varalāṟu
‘kolyāṉi pala ōṭṭik kūṭāmaṉṉar kuḻām tavirtta palyāka mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti eṉṉum pāṇṭiyāti rājaṉāl nākamāmalarc cōli naḷirciṉi mici vaṇṭalampum pākaṉūrk kūṟṟam eṉṉum paḻaṉak kiṭakki nīrnāṭṭuc coṟkaṇṇāḷar colappaṭṭa curuti mārkkam piḻiyāta koṟkikiḻāṉ naṟkoṟṟāṉ koṇṭa vēḷvi muṟṟuvikkak kēḷvi antaṇāḷar muṉ ‘kēṭka’ eṉṟeṭutturittu vēḷvik cāli muṉpu niṉṟu ‘vēḷvikkuṭi’ eṉṟu appatiyic cīrōṭu tiruvaḷarac ceytār vēntaṉ. appoḻutēy nīrōṭu aṭṭik koṭuttamiyāl nīṭu pukti tuyttapiṉ aḷavariya ātirājari akala nīkki akaliṭattik kaḷapraṉ eṉṉum kaliyaricaṉ kikkoṇṭataṉi iṟakkiya piṉ-

vikrama pārakaṉ vīrapurōkaṉ
marutpalaṉ māṉya cācaṉaṉ
maṉupamaṉ mārttita vīraṉ
kiristiraṉ kītikiṉṉaraṉ
kripālayaṉ kritāpatāṉaṉ
kalippaki kaṇṭaka niṣṭūraṉ
kārya tatṣiṇaṉ kārmuka pārttaṉ
parāntakaṉ paṇṭita vatsalaṉ
paripūrṇaṉ pāpapīru
kuriyaṟu kaṭal paṭittāṉi kuṇakrihyaṉ kūṭa nirṇayaṉ
niriyuṟu malarmaṇi nīṇmuṭi nēriyarkōṉ neṭuñcaṭiyaṉ
maṟṟavaṉ taṉ rājya vatsaram mūṉṟāvatu celā niṟpa
paḻiya koṭikkup putiya urimi
	āṅku orunāḷ
māṭa māmatil kūṭal
pāṭu niṉṟavar ākrōtikkak
koṟṟavaṉē maṟṟavarit
teṟṟeṉa naṉku kūvi,
‘eṉṉēy numkuṟi’ eṉṟu 
muṉṉākap paṇittaruḷa 
“mēlnāḷ niṉkuravarāl
pāl muṟiyiṉ vaḻuvāmi
mākam tōymalarc cōlip
pākaṉūrk kūṟṟattup paṭuvatu
āḷvatāṉi aṭalvēntēy
‘vēḷvikuṭi’ eṉṉum piyaruṭiyatu
olkāta vēltāṉiyōṭu otavēli uṭaṉkātta
‘palyāka mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti’ eṉṉum paramēsvaraṉāl
vēḷvikuṭi eṉṉap paṭṭatu
kēḷviyil tarappaṭṭataṉi
tuḷakkam illāk kaṭaltāṉiyāy
‘kaḷaprarāl’ iṟakkappaṭṭatu
eṉṟu niṉṟu avaṉ vijñāpyañ ceyya
‘naṉṟu naṉṟu’ eṉṟu muṟuvalittu,
‘nāṭṭāl niṉpaḻami yātal kāṭṭi nī koḷka’ eṉṉa
nāṭṭāl taṉpaḻami yātal kāṭṭiṉāṉ aṅkap
poḻutēy kāṭṭa
“mēṉāḷ eṅkuravarāl
pāṉmuṟiyiṉ tarappaṭṭati
emmālum tarappaṭṭatu” eṉṟu
cemmāntu avaṉ eṭuttaruḷi
viṟkit taṭakki viṟalvēntaṉ
koṟki kiḻāṉ kāmak kāṇi naṟciṅkaṟkut
tērōṭuṅ kaṭaltāṉiyāṉ
nīrōṭu aṭṭuk koṭuttamiyiṉ
elli
maṟṟitaṟkup perunāṉku elli
teṟṟeṉa virittu rippiṉ
pukaraṟu poḻiṉ maruṅkuṭutta
nakarūr ellikkum mēkkum
maṟṟitaṟkut teṉ ṉelli
kuḷantivaṅ kūḻvanti ceykkum
kaḷantik kuḷattil ālukku vaṭakkum
maṟṟitaṟku mēl elli
aṟṟam illāk
koṟṟaṉ puttūr oṭumi
iruppic ceyyiṭi mēltalip
peruppiṟkuk kiḻakkum
maṟṟitaṟku vaṭapāl elli
kāyaluḷ kamalam malarum
pāyaluḷ vaṭapālip peruppiṟkut teṟkum
urimi
	ivviyitta perunāṉku elliyil paṭṭapūmi           kārāṇmi, mīyāṭci
uḷḷaṭaṅka mēl eṅkuravarāl kuṭukkappaṭṭa paricēy emmālum
koṭukkap paṭṭatu.
āṇatti māṟaṅkāri
	maṟṟitaṟku āṇatti
kuṟṟamiṉṟik kūṟuṅkālik
koṅkarava naṟuṅkaṇṇik
kaṅkarājaṉatu kanyā ratṉam
koṅkar kōṟkuk kuṇantu koṭuppa
ārppaṟā aṭaltāṉip
pūrvarājar pukaṉṟeḻuntu
vilviravum kaṭaltāṉi
vallavaṉi veṇpivāy
āḷ amaruḷ aḻintōṭa
vāḷ amaruḷ uṭaṉvavviya
ēṉappoṟi ikal amaruḷ iṭiyurum eṉa valaṉēnti
malitta tāṉi matavikalaṉ maṉṉarkōṉ aruḷiṟ peṟṟum
kolvaḷikkum vēltāṉip
palvaḷik kōṉ kuṇarappaṭṭu
poravantavar matam tavirkkum
karavanta purattavar kulattōṉṟal
māvēntuṅ kaṭal tāṉi
mūvēnta maṅkalap pērariyaṉākiya
vitya cikāmaṇi māṟaṅkāri
paṅkīṭum cācaṉam eḻututalum
	ippiramatēyam uṭiya koṟkikiḻāṉ kāmakkāṇi cuvaraṉ ciṅkaṉ
itaṉuḷ mūṉṟil oṉṟun taṉakku vittu iraṇṭu kūṟum aimpatiṉavar
prāhmaṇark kunīrōṭu aṭṭik koṭuttāṉ
itaṉuḷ mūrtti eyiṉaṉ caviyōṭu ottatu
nāṉkarip paṭākāram uṭiyaṉa
itaṉuḷ taṉakku vitta oru kūṟṟilum tampimārkku
nāṉkum, tamciṟṟappaṉār makkaḷukku āṟum
capiyōṭu otta paṭākāram koṭuttāṉ
ippiracasti pāṭiṉa cēṉāpati ēṉāti āyiṉa cāttañ cāttaṟku mūṉṟu kūṟṟārumāyt taṅkaḷōṭu
otta nāṉku paṭākāram koṭuttār
‘maṟṟitaṉik kāttār malaraṭi eṉmuṭimēla’ eṉṟu
koṟṟavaṉē paṇit taruḷit teṟṟeṉat
tāmra cācaṉañ ceyvittāṉ
iytu eḻutiṉa cutta kēcarip perumpaṇikkāraṉukku
perumakkaḷ aruḷāṟ peṟṟatu oru
illa vaḷāvum iraṇṭumāc ceyyum
oru puṉceyyum peṟṟāṉ
ivi yutta kēcarip perumpaṇikkāraṉ eḻuttu.

kaḷappirar āṭciyil tamiḻakam
mayili. cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi (1975)

mukavuri
tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ varalāṟu muḻumiyākavum teḷivākavum cariyākavum iṉṉum eḻutappaṭāmaliruppatu tamiḻariṉ peruṅkuṟi yākum. tamiḻakattiṉ mika nīṇṭa varalāṟṟil kaḷappirariṉ āṭcik kālam mukkiyamāṉatu. kaḷappirar āṭcikkālam tamiḻ nāṭṭu varalāṟṟil ‘iruṇṭa kālam’ āka iruntu vantatu. oru kālattil kaḷappirar tamiḻakatti aracāṇṭārkaḷ eṉṉum varalāṟṟucceytiyē neṭuṅ kālamāka maṟintu kiṭantatu. vēḷvikkuṭic ceppēṭu kiṭittu, itu veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa piṟakutāṉ kaḷappirar eṉṉum aracar iruntaṉar eṉṟum avarkaḷ tamiḻakatti aracāṇṭaṉar eṉṟum teriya vantatu. ataṟku muṉ appaṭi oru aracar parampari iruntatu eṉpati aṟiyāmalē iruntōm.
1920 ām āṇṭukkup piṟaku vēḷvikkuṭic ceppēṭṭu vācakam veḷivanta piṟaku 55 āṇṭukaḷākattāṉ kaḷappirarip paṟṟik koñcaṅkoñcamāka aṟintu varukiṟōm. aimpat tintu āṇṭu kaḷākiyum iṉṉum avarkaḷip paṟṟiya muḻu varalāṟu teriyā malirukkiṟatu. aṟiñarkaḷ kaḷappirarip paṟṟic cila kaṭṭurikaḷ eḻutiṉārkaḷ. cila varalāṟṟup pērāciriyarkaḷ taṅkaḷuṭiya varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷil kaḷappirariṉ ‘iruṇṭa kālatti’ orē variyil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷaṉar. avvaḷavutāṉ.
kaḷappirar varalāṟu eḻutappaṭātataṉ kāraṇam, avarkaḷ kālattuk kalveṭṭuc cācaṉaṅkaḷō, ceppēṭṭuc cācaṉaṅkaḷō, avarkaḷ kālattuk kācukaḷō, vēṟu paḻamporuḷ cāṉṟukaḷō kiṭikkātatutāṉ. inta niliyil avarkaḷip paṟṟi aṟiyakkūṭiya orē cāṉṟu akkālattuc camaya, ilakkiya nūlkaḷēyākum. intac cāṉṟukaḷi ituvariyil yārum atikamākak kiyāḷavilli. periya purāṇamum yāpparuṅkala uri mēṟkōḷ ceyyuṭkaḷum ōraḷavu payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. ituvari veḷivanta kaḷappirarip paṟṟiya kaṭṭurikaḷum avarkaḷ varalāṟṟi ōraḷavē terivik kiṉṟaṉa.
akkālattil irunta (civa, viṇava, putta, camaṇa) camayaṅkaḷiṉ varalāṟu, camaya nūlkaḷiṉ (patiṉōrām tirumuṟi) varalāṟu, ceyyuḷ ilakkaṇa (yāpparuṅkalam) varalāṟu ākiya vaṟṟip paṟṟi ārāyntapōtu kaḷappirar āṭcik kālattip paṟṟic cila ceytikaḷ putitākap pulaṉāyiṉa. ākavē, innūli eḻutiṉēṉ. kaḷappirar kālattil naṭanta pala nikaḻccikaḷ inta nūlil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa eṉpati innūlip paṭippavar aṟintukoḷḷalām.
kaḷappirariṉ ‘iruṇṭakālam’ inta nūliṉāl ‘viṭiyaṟ kālam’ ākiṟatu. mēlum putiporuḷ cāṉṟukaḷ, paḻaṅkālac cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭikkumāṉāl kaḷappirar varalāṟṟil pakaṟ kālattik kāṇak kūṭum.
vāḻkkiyil ēṟpaṭṭa maṉakkavaliyum tuṉpamum nōyum toṭarntu varuttukiṟa kālattil innūli eḻutiṉēṉ.
innūl veḷivara muḻu muyaṟcikaḷ eṭuttukkoṇṭa ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tolporuḷ tuṟi āyvu māṇavar tiru. ra. rāmacāmi avarkaḷukku eṉ naṉṟi uriyatākum.
inta nūli acciṭṭu veḷiyiṭum makkaḷ veḷiyīṭu niṟuvaṉattic cērnta tiru. mē. tu. rācukumār avarkaḷukku eṉṉuṭiya naṉṟiyit terivikkak kaṭamippaṭṭuḷḷēṉ.
mayilāppūr mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi ceṉṉi - 4 20-10-1975

tōṟṟuvāy
kaṭiccaṅka kālattukkup piṟaku tamiḻakattiṉ araciyal nilimi iṉṉateṉṟu teriyāmal carittirattil ‘iruṇṭa kāla’ māka iruntatu. kaṭiccaṅka kālattiṉ muṭivu ēṟattāḻa ki. pi. 250 eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu. kaṭiccaṅka kālattiṉ iṟutiyil irunta cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷi liruntu kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. caṅka nūlkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṟa kaṭici cēra aracaṉ peyar kōkkōtimārpaṉ eṉpatu. ivaṉik kōti eṉṟu kūṟuvar. kōkkōti mārpaṉ, cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉuṭiya makaṉ. ivaṉ ciṟuvaṉāka iruntapōtu ivaṉukkuk kuṭṭuvaṉ cēral eṉṟu peyar iruntatu. taṉ mēl patiṟṟuppattu aintām pattup pāṭiya paraṇarukkuc ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ umparkāṭu eṉṉum nāṭṭiṉ varuvāyiyum taṉṉuṭiya makaṉ kuṭṭuvaṉ cēraliyum paricākak koṭuttāṉ. (patiṟṟuppattu, aintām pattup patikattiṉ aṭikkuṟippu).
pulavarākiya paraṇarukkuc ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ taṉṉuṭiya makaṉāṉa kuṭṭuvaṉ cēralip paricākak koṭuttāṉ eṉpataṉ poruḷ, pulavariṭattil kalvi kaṟpataṟku māṇākkaṉākak koṭuttāṉ eṉpatākum. iḷamiyil kuṭṭuvaṉ cēral eṉṟu peyar peṟṟirunta ivaṉ muṭicūṭiṉa piṟaku kōkkōti mārpaṉ eṉṟu peyar peṟṟāṉ eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm.
kaṭiccaṅka kālattil koṅku nāṭṭic cēra aracar kaḷiṉ iḷiya vaḻiyiṉarāṉa poṟiyar aracāṇṭārkaḷ. koṅku nāṭṭi āṇṭa kaṭicip poṟiyaṉ kaṇikkāl irumpoṟi. ivaṉ kōkkōti mārpaṉuṭiya tāyāti uṟaviṉaṉ. iruvarum cama kālattil iruntavarkaḷ. kaṇikkālirumpoṟi, cōḻaṉ ceṅkaṇāṉōṭu pōr ceytu tōṟṟup pōril piṭikkappaṭṭaṭuc ciṟi vikkappaṭṭāṉ. piṟaku ciṟiyiliruntu viṭuvikkappaṭṭu mīṇṭum koṅku nāṭṭiyaracāṇṭāṉ. atāvatu, cōḻaṉ ceṅkaṇāṉukkuk kīḻaṭaṅkik kaṇikkālirumpoṟi aracāṇṭaṉāṉ. ākavē, cōḻaṉ ceṅkāṇāṉum cēramāṉ kōkkōti mārpaṉum kaṇikkāl irumpoṟiyum camakālattavar eṉṟu terikiṉṟaṉar.
kaṭiccaṅka kālattiṉ iṟutiyil pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiyara cāṇṭavaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ. inta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ, kōvalaṉ kaṇṇaki kālattilirunta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ allaṉ. avaṉukkup piṟaku irunta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ. ivaṉit taliyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉ eṉṟu varalāṟu kūṟukiṟatu. inta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ, cēra nāṭṭuk kaṭaṟkariyōrattil pēriyāṟṟiṉ pukumukattil irunta muciṟippaṭṭiṉatti muṟṟukiceytu aṅkirunta oru teyvat tirumēṉiyi eṭuttukkoṇṭu maturikkup pōṉāṉ (akanāṉūṟu 57: 14-16; 11-14). ivaṉuṭiya camakālattilirunta cēra aracaṉ mēṟcoṉṉa kōkkōtimārpaṉē.
